{D160}]@##/_/rgya gar skad du/_Ar+ya brah+ma bi she Sha tsin ti pa ri pr-its+tsha nA ma ma hA yA na sU tra/_bod skad du/_'phags pa tshangs pa khyad par sems kyis zhus pa zhes bya ba theg pa chen po'i mdo/_bam po dang po/_sangs rgyas dang byang chub sems dpa' thams cad la phyag 'tshal 
[\[23a.2\]]lo/_/'di skad bdag gis thos pa dus gcig na/_bcom ldan 'das rgyal po'i khab bya ka lan da ka gnas pa'i 'od ma'i tshal na dge slong stong phrag drug cu rtsa bzhi'i dge slong gi dge 'dun chen po dang /_byang chub sems dpa' stong phrag bdun cu rtsa gnyis dang thabs cig tu 
[\[23a.3\]]bzhugs te/_thams cad kyang mngon par shes pas mngon par shes pa/_gzungs thob pa/_spobs pa thogs pa med pa/_ting nge 'dzin thob pa/_mngon par shes pas rnam par rol cing spobs pa mi 'jigs pa dang ldan pa/_chos thams cad kyi ngo bo nyid ston pa la 
[\[23a.4\]]mkhas pa mi skye ba'i chos la bzod pa thob pa rnams la 'di lta ste/_gzhon nur gyur pa 'jam dpal dang /_gzhon nur gyur pa lag na rin po che dang /_gzhon nur gyur pa lag na phyag rgya rin po che dang /_gzhon nur gyur pa rin po che dpal dang /_gzhon nur gyur pa nam mkha' mdzod dang /_
[\[23a.5\]]gzhon nur gyur pa sems bskyed ma thag tu chos kyi 'khor lo bskor ba dang /_gzhon nur gyur pa dra ba can gyi 'od dang /_gzhon nur gyur pa rnam par bsgyings pa dang /_gzhon nur gyur pa dpal gyi snying po dang /_gzhon nur gyur pa bdog pa thams cad yongs su gtong ba dang /_
[\[23a.6\]]gzhon nur gyur pa pad ma bkod pa dang /_gzhon nur gyur pa seng ge dang /_gzhon nur gyur pa zla 'od dang /_gzhon nur gyur pa zla ba'i 'od zer gyi 'od dang /_gzhon nur gyur pa blo gros mchog dang /_gzhon nur gyur pa rgyan thams cad bkod pa dang /_bzang skyong la sogs pa skyes 
[\[23a.7\]]bu dam pa bcu drug la 'di lta ste/_bzang skyong dang /_dkon mchog 'byung gnas dang /_ded dpon mchog dang /_mes byin dang /_phug sbas dang /_chus byin dang /_dbang pos byin dang /_bla ma'i blo gros dang /_khyad par blo gros dang /_blo gros 'phel dang /_mthong ba 
[\[23b\]]
[\[23b.1\]]don yod dang /_shin tu yang dag zhugs dang /_rab kyi rtsal gyis rnam par gnon pa dang /_blo gros mtha' yas pa dang /_brtson pa mi gtong ba dang /_nyi ma'i snying po dang /_sa 'dzin dang /_de lta bu la sogs pa byang chub sems dpa' stong phrag bdun cu rtsa gnyis dang /_
[\[23b.2\]]rgyal po chen po bzhi dang /_brgya byin lha'i dbang po la sogs pa sum cu rtsa gsum pa'i lha rnams dang /_mtshe ma dang /_dga' ldan dang /_'phrul dga' dang /_gzhan 'phrul dbang byed kyi lha rnams dang /_tshangs pa la sogs pa tshangs pa'i ris kyi lha brgya stong 
[\[23b.3\]]dang /_de ma yin pa gzhan gzi brjid che ba'i lha dang /_klu dang /_gnod sbyin dang /_dri za dang /_lha ma yin dang /_nam mkha' lding dang /_mi 'am ci dang /_lto 'phye chen po dang /_mi dang mi ma yin pa rnams dang yang thabs cig ste/_de nas bcom ldan 'das 'khor brgya stong 
[\[23b.4\]]du mas bskor cing mdun du bdar nas chos ston to/_/de nas byang chub sems dpa' gzhon nur gyur pa dra ba can gyi 'od bla gos phrag pa gcig tu gzar te/_pus mo g.yas pa'i lha nga sa la btsugs nas bcom ldan 'das ga la ba de logs su thal mo sbyar ba btud de/_
[\[23b.5\]]stong gsum gyi stong chen po'i 'jig rten gyi khams rab tu g.yo bar byas nas/_stong gsum gyi stong chen po'i 'jig rten gyi khams kyi sems can thams cad la bltas nas bcom ldan 'das la 'di skad ces gsol to/_/gal te zhus nas zhu ba lung bstan pa'i slad du bcom 
[\[23b.6\]]ldan 'das kyis bdag la skabs phye na/_bdag bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas la phyogs 'ga' zhig zhu'o/_/de skad ces gsol pa dang /_bcom ldan 'das kyis byang chub sems dpa' dra ba can gyi 'od la 
[\[23b.7\]]'di skad ces bka' stsal to/_/dra ba can gyi 'od khyod ci dang ci 'dod pa de bzhin gshegs pa la dris shig dang /_dris pa de dang de nyid lung bstan pas ngas khyod kyi sems rangs par bya'o/_/de skad ces bka' stsal pa dang /_bcom ldan 'das la byang chub sems dpa' dra ba 
[\[24a\]]
[\[24a.1\]]can gyi 'od kyis 'di skad ces gsol to/_/bcom ldan 'das kyi sku ni blta bas chog mi shes pa kha dog nyi ma bye ba khrag khrig brgya stong bas lhag pa lags te/_bcom ldan 'das bdag ni de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku blta ba'am brtag par nus 
[\[24a.2\]]pa 'di ni ngo mtshar to snyam bgyid do/_/bcom ldan 'das yang bdag ni de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku blta ba'am rtog pa de dag thams cad ni sangs rgyas kyi mthu'o snyam bgyid do/_/bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/_dra ba can gyi 'od ji skad zer ba de bzhin te/_de 
[\[24a.3\]]bzhin gshegs pas skabs ma phye bar de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku blta ba'am brtag par mi nus so/_/de ci'i phyir zhe na/_dra ba can gyi 'od de bzhin gshegs pa la rab tu zhi ba'i rnam par bkod pa zhes bya ba'i 'od zer yod de/_'od zer des reg pa'i sems can rnams 
[\[24a.4\]]de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku la yang blta zhing rtog la de dag gi mig gi dbang po yang nyams par mi 'gyur ro/_/dra ba can gyi 'od de bzhin gshegs pa la mi 'jigs pa spobs pa zhes bya ba'i 'od zer yod de/_'od zer des reg pa'i sems can rnams de bzhin gshegs 
[\[24a.5\]]pa la kun 'dri zhing phyir 'dri bar byed do/_/dra ba can gyi 'od de bzhin gshegs pa la dge ba'i rtsa ba bsags pa zhes bya ba'i 'od zer yod de/_'od zer des reg pa'i sems can rnams 'khor los sgyur ba'i rgyal po'i rgyal srid thob par bya ba'i phyir de bzhin gshegs pa la kun 
[\[24a.6\]]'dri'o/_/de bzhin du sbyar te/_'od zer dge ba'i rnam par bkod pa zhes bya ba'i 'od zer des reg pa'i sems can rnams brgya byin thob par bya ba'i phyir de bzhin gshegs pa la kun 'dri'o/_/'od zer bdag po thob pa zhes bya ba'i 'od zer des reg pa'i sems can rnams tshangs 
[\[24a.7\]]pa'i 'jig rten du skye bar bya ba'i phyir de bzhin gshegs pa la kun 'dri'o/_/'od zer nyon mongs pa med par gyur pa zhes bya ba'i 'od zer des reg pa'i sems can rnams de bzhin gshegs pa la nyan thos kyi theg pa kun 'dri'o/_/'od zer rtse gcig pa dben pa dang ldan pa 
[\[24b\]]
[\[24b.1\]]zhes bya ba'i 'od zer des reg pa'i sems can rnams de bzhin gshegs pa la rang sangs rgyas kyi theg pa kun 'dri'o/_/'od zer thams cad mkhyen pa'i ye shes su dbang bskur ba thob pa zhes bya ba'i 'od zer des reg pa'i sems can rnams de bzhin gshegs pa la 
[\[24b.2\]]theg pa chen po sangs rgyas kyi theg pa kun 'dri'o/_/'od zer khyad par du 'gro ba zhes bya ba'i 'od zer des de bzhin gshegs pa byon pa'am bzhud pa'i zhabs kyi mthil gyis sems can gang la reg pa de dag shi 'phos ma thag tu bde 'gro mtho ris kyi 'jig rten du skye'o/_/
[\[24b.3\]]rgyan thams cad bkod pa zhes bya ba'i 'od zer de/_de bzhin gshegs pa grong du gshegs pa na 'byin te/_der de bzhin gshegs pa'i 'od des sems can su dag la reg pa de dag thams cad bde ba'i mchog dang ldan par 'gyur ro/_/rgyan thams cad bkod pa yang 
[\[24b.4\]]grong khyer der 'byung bar 'gyur ro/_/rnam par 'thor ba zhes bya ba'i 'od zer des ni de bzhin gshegs pa 'jig rten gyi khams mtha' yas mu med pa rab tu g.yo bar byed do/_/bde ba ston pa zhes bya ba'i 'od zer des ni sems can dmyal ba pa'i sems can rnams kyi sems 
[\[24b.5\]]can dmyal ba'i sdug bsngal med par byed do/_/'od zer byams pas 'phags pa zhes bya ba des ni byol song gi skye gnas su song ba'i sems can rnams gcig la gcig mi za bar byed do/_/'od zer rab tu sim par byed pa zhes bya ba des ni gshin rje'i 'jig rten pa'i 
[\[24b.6\]]sems can rnams kyi gshin rje'i 'jig rten gyi sdug bsngal med par byed do/_/dri ma med pa zhes bya ba'i 'od zer des ni sems can ldongs pa rnams mig thob par 'gyur ro/_/rna ba'i rjes su 'thun pa zhes bya ba'i 'od zer des ni sems can 'on pa rnams rna ba thob par 
[\[24b.7\]]'gyur ro/_/rnam par spong ba zhes bya ba'i 'od zer des ni sems can mi dge ba bcu'i las kyi lam la gnas pa rnams dge ba bcu'i las kyi lam thob par 'gyur ro/_/ngo tsha shes pa zhes bya ba'i 'od zer des ni sems can myos pa rnams dran pa 'thob par 'gyur ro/_/
[\[25a\]]
[\[25a.1\]]med par 'gyur ba zhes bya ba'i 'od zer des ni sems can log par lta ba can rnams yang dag par lta ba thob par 'gyur ro/_/rab tu gtong ba zhes bya ba'i 'od zer des ni sems can ser sna can rnams gtong ba thob par 'gyur ro/_/yongs su gdung ba 
[\[25a.2\]]med pa zhes bya ba'i 'od zer des ni sems can ngan pa'i ngang tshul can rnams tshul khrims thob par 'gyur ro/_/phan pa dang ldan pa zhes bya ba'i 'od zer des ni sems can gnod par sems pa rnams bzod pa dang des pa thob par 'gyur ro/_/'bar ba can zhes bya ba'i 
[\[25a.3\]]'od zer des ni sems can le lo can rnams brtson 'grus thob par 'gyur ro/_/rtse gcig pa zhes bya ba'i 'od zer des ni sems can dran pa nyams pa rnams bsam gtan thob par 'gyur ro/_/ye shes zhes bya ba'i 'od zer des ni sems can 'chal pa'i shes rab 
[\[25a.4\]]can rnams shes rab thob par 'gyur ro/_/rnyog pa med pa zhes bya ba'i 'od zer des ni sems can ma dad pa rnams dad pa thob par 'gyur ro/_/kun 'dzin pa zhes bya ba'i 'od zer des ni sems can thos pa nyung ba rnams thos pa thob par 'gyur ro/_/spyod pa dang ldan 
[\[25a.5\]]pa zhes bya ba'i 'od zer des ni sems can ngo tsha ba dang 'dzem pa med pa rnams ngo tsha shes pa dang /_khrel yod pa thob par 'gyur ro/_/skyo ba zhes bya ba'i 'od zer des ni sems can 'dod chags spyod pa rnams kyi 'dod chags 'joms par 'gyur ro/_/dga' ba zhes bya 
[\[25a.6\]]ba'i 'od zer des ni sems can zhe sdang spyod pa rnams kyi zhe sdang 'joms par 'gyur ro/_/snang ba can zhes bya ba'i 'od zer des ni sems can gti mug spyod pa rnams rten cing 'brel par 'byung ba khong du chud par 'gyur ro/_/kun tu 'gro ba zhes bya ba'i 'od zer des ni sems 
[\[25a.7\]]can cha mnyam pa spyod pa rnams cha mnyam pa spyod pa spong bar 'gyur ro/_/dra ba can gyi 'od de bzhin gshegs pa la gzugs thams cad ston pa zhes bya ba'i 'od zer yod de/_'od zer des reg pa'i sems can rnams de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku kha dog sna tshogs kha dog du ma/_
[\[25b\]]
[\[25b.1\]]kha dog brgya stong du mas lhag par mthong bar 'gyur ro/_/dra ba can gyi 'od ngas de bzhin gshegs pa'i 'od zer la brtsams te/_bskal pa'am/_bskal pa las lhag par bshad kyang de bzhin gshegs pa'i 'od zer gyi rnam pa dang ldan pa'i chos bstan pa yongs 
[\[25b.2\]]su zad par mi 'gyur te/_de ltar de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas ni 'od zer gyi yon tan mtha' yas mu med pa dang ldan pa yin no/_/de skad ces bka' stsal pa dang /_bcom ldan 'das la byang chub sems dpa' dra ba can 
[\[25b.3\]]gyi 'od kyis 'di skad ces gsol to/_/bcom ldan 'das 'di ltar de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku'i bkod pa tshad ma mchis par bstan pa 'di dang /_thabs mkhas pa bsam gyis mi khyab pa dang ldan pa'i chos bstan pa ngo mtshar to/_/bcom ldan 'das 'od zer 
[\[25b.4\]]'di dag ni bdag cag gis sngon ma thos te/_ji lta ji ltar bcom ldan 'das kyis bshad pa'i don bdag gis 'tshal ba ltar na/_bcom ldan 'das byang chub sems dpa' gang la la 'od zer 'di dag gi ming thos nas dad pa rnyed par 'gyur ba de dag thams cad yang dag 
[\[25b.5\]]par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas kyi 'od zer 'di lta bus rab tu snang bar bgyid pa'i mthu dang ldan par 'gyur ro/_/yang bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa 'od zer gang gis sangs rgyas kyi zhing gzhan gyi byang chub sems dpa' rnams bskul ma thag tu mi mjed kyi 
[\[25b.6\]]'jig rten gyi khams 'dir mchi bar 'gyur ba dang /_byang chub sems dpa' su dag 'dri ba dang /_lung bstan pa dang /_bshad pa la mthar phyin pa rnams mchis nas de bzhin gshegs pa la kun zhu zhing bsnyen bkur bgyid pa dang /_slar zhu bar 'gyur ba'i 'od zer 
[\[25b.7\]]byang chub sems dpa' bskul ba zhes bgyi ba gtang du gsol/_de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis de'i tshe 'od gang gis 'jig rten gyi khams mtha' yas mu med par snang bar 'gyur ba'i 'od zer de lta bu sku las phyung ste/_byang chub sems dpa' rnams 'od des 
[\[26a\]]
[\[26a.1\]]bskul ma thag tu mi mjed kyi 'jig rten gyi khams 'dir phyin par gyur to/_/de nas sangs rgyas kyi zhing 'di nas shar phyogs su sangs rgyas kyi zhing brgya stong phrag gang gA'i klung bdun cu rtsa gnyis kyi bye ma snyed 'das pa na yongs su dag pa zhes bya 
[\[26a.2\]]ba'i 'jig rten gyi khams yod de/_de na zla ba'i 'od ces bya ba de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas da ltar bzhugs la 'tsho zhing gzhes te/_byang chub sems dpa' 'ba' zhig la chos kyang 'chad pa'i sangs rgyas kyi zhing de nas 
[\[26a.3\]]phyir mi ldog pa'i byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po tshangs pa khyad par sems zhes bya ba de 'od des bskul ma thag tu bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa zla ba'i 'od ga la ba der song ste phyin pa dang /_bcom ldan 'das de'i zhabs la mgo bos phyag 
[\[26a.4\]]'tshal nas/_bcom ldan 'das de la 'di skad ces gsol to/_/bcom ldan 'das 'jig rten du snang ba chen po 'di byung ba'i rgyu gang lags/_rkyen gang lags/_bcom ldan 'das des 'di skad ces bka' stsal to/_/tshangs pa sangs rgyas kyi zhing 'di 
[\[26a.5\]]nas nub phyogs na sangs rgyas kyi zhing brgya stong phrag gang gA'i klung bdun cu rtsa gnyis kyi bye ma snyed 'das pa na mi mjed ces bya ba'i 'jig rten gyi khams yod de/_de na shAkya thub pa zhes bya ba de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs 
[\[26a.6\]]rgyas da ltar bzhugs te/_'tsho zhing gzhes la chos kyang ston to/_/de bzhin gshegs pa des 'di lta ste/_phyogs bcu'i byang chub sems dpa' rnams bsdu ba'i phyir sku las 'od 'di gtang ngo /_/de nas bcom ldan 'das la tshangs pa khyad par sems 
[\[26a.7\]]kyis 'di skad ces gsol to/_/bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa de bdag gzigs par bzhed na mi mjed kyi 'jig rten gyi khams der bcom ldan 'das shAkya thub pa de la lta ba dang /_phyag 'tshal ba dang /_bsnyen bkur bgyi ba dang /_kun zhu ba dang /_
[\[26b\]]
[\[26b.1\]]slar zhu ba'i slad du bdag mchi'o/_/bcom ldan 'das des bka' stsal pa/_tshangs pa da ltar mi mjed kyi 'jig rten gyi khams der byang chub sems dpa' bye ba phrag mang po yang phyin gyis de'i dus la dbab par shes na khyod song shig_/tshangs pa sangs rgyas kyi 
[\[26b.2\]]zhing der lhag pa'i bsam pa bcu rnams kyis gnas par bya'o/_/bcu gang zhe na/_snyan par smra ba dang /_ngan du smra ba rnams la khong khro ba med pa'i sems dang /_snyan pa thos pa dang /_mi snyan pa thos pa la byams pa'i sems dang /_dus thams cad du sems 
[\[26b.3\]]can rnams la snying rje ba'i sems dang /_sems can rab 'bring tha ma rnams la yang dag par sbyor ba dang /_ri mo byed pa dang /_brnyas par byed pa rnams la sems mnyam pa dang /_gzhan gyi ltung ba'i 'khrul pa thams cad la nyes par mi lta ba dang /_theg pa 
[\[26b.4\]]sna tshogs la ro gcig pa dang /_ngan song gi sdug bsngal thams cad kyi sgras mi skrag pa dang /_byang chub sems dpa' thams cad la ston par 'du shes pa dang /_sangs rgyas kyi zhing snyigs ma lnga can na de bzhin gshegs pa mthong ba la ngo mtshar du 'du shes pa ste/_
[\[26b.5\]]tshangs pa lhag pa'i bsam pa bcu po de dag gis sangs rgyas kyi zhing der gnas par bya'o/_/de nas bcom ldan 'das la tshangs pa khyad par sems kyis 'di skad ces gsol to/_/bcom ldan 'das kyi spyan sngar bdag seng ge'i sgra mi sgrog ste/_'on kyang 
[\[26b.6\]]bdag gis spyod pa gang lags pa'i spyod pa 'di la bcom ldan 'das nyid mngon sum du gyur pas bcom ldan 'das bdag gis lhag pa'i bsam pa de dag kyang rnam par sbyang zhing mnyam par gzhag pa'i bye brag gis kyang sangs rgyas kyi zhing der gnas par bgyi'o/_/
[\[26b.7\]]de nas bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa zla 'od kyi sangs rgyas kyi zhing de'i byang chub sems dpa' gzhan dag gis kyang bcom ldan 'das la 'di skad ces gsol to/_/bcom ldan 'das de ltar sems can ma rungs pa rnams tshogs pa'i sangs rgyas kyi zhing der 
[\[27a\]]
[\[27a.1\]]bdag cag ma skyes pa ni rnyed pa legs par rnyed do/_/bcom ldan 'das des 'di skad ces bka' stsal to/_/rigs kyi bu khyed rnams de skad ces ma 'dzer cig_/de ci'i phyir zhe na/_rigs kyi bu dag sangs rgyas kyi zhing 'di na gang bskal pa brgya stong du tshangs par 
[\[27a.2\]]spyod pa la gnas pa bas mi mjed kyi 'jig rten gyi khams na gang snga dro'i dus gcig sems zhe sdang med par gnas na de bas de shin tu mchog go/_/de ci'i phyir zhe na/_'jig rten gyi khams de ni de tsam du kun nas nyon mongs par gyur pa ste/_sems can de dag kyang de tsam du 
[\[27a.3\]]gtse ba mang zhing kun nas nyon mongs pa mang ba yin no/_/de nas sangs rgyas kyi zhing de nas byang chub sems dpa' stong phrag bcu gnyis tshangs pa khyad par sems dang lhan cig tu chas te/_tshangs pa bdag cag kyang mi mjed kyi 'jig rten gyi khams der mchi'o/_/
[\[27a.4\]]bsam pa 'di dag kyang rnam par sbyong ngo /_/bcom ldan 'das shAkya thub pa de la yang blta'o/_/phyag bgyi'o/_/bsnyen bkur bgyi'o/_/mnyam par gzhag pa'i bye brag gis kyang sangs rgyas kyi zhing der gnas par bgyi'o/_/de nas tshangs pa khyad par sems byang 
[\[27a.5\]]chub sems dpa' stong phrag bcu gnyis dang lhan cig tu bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa zla 'od kyi zhabs la mgo bos phyag 'tshal nas 'di lta ste/_skyes bu stobs dang ldan pa'i lag pa bskum pa las brkyang ba'am/_brkyang ba las bskum pa'i skad cig yud 
[\[27a.6\]]tsam thang cig de tsam gyis sangs rgyas kyi zhing der mi snang bar gyur nas bcom ldan 'das shAkya thub pa de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas kyi zhing 'dir phyin to/_/de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis byang chub sems dpa' dra ba can gyi 
[\[27a.7\]]'od la bka' stsal pa/_dra ba can gyi 'od khyod kyis tshangs pa khyad par sems 'ong ba mthong ngam/_gsol pa/_bcom ldan 'das mthong ngo /_/bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/_dra ba can gyi 'od tshangs pa khyad par sems 'di ni byang chub sems dpa' tshul 
[\[27b\]]
[\[27b.1\]]bzhin 'dri ba/_kun 'dri ba la mkhas pa rnams kyi nang na mchog go/_/tshig gzung ba rnams kyi nang na mchog go/_/tshig btsun pa rnams kyi nang na mchog go/_/tshig 'jam pa rnams kyi nang na mchog go/_/gsong por smra ba rnams kyi nang na mchog go/_/tshig mchod 
[\[27b.2\]]pa rnams kyi nang na mchog go/_/tshig thogs pa med pa rnams kyi nang na mchog go/_/ldem po ngag tu smra ba rnams kyi nang na mchog go/_/sems khong khro ba med pa rnams kyi nang na mchog go/_/byams pa la gnas pa rnams kyi nang na mchog go/_/snying rje la gnas pa rnams 
[\[27b.3\]]kyi nang na mchog go/_/dga' ba la gnas pa rnams kyi nang na mchog go/_/btang snyoms la gnas pa rnams kyi nang na mchog go/_/byang chub sems dpa' the tsom thams cad gcod pa dang kun 'dri ba la mkhas pa rnams kyi nang na mchog go/_/de nas tshangs pa khyad par 
[\[27b.4\]]sems dang /_byang chub sems dpa' stong phrag bcu gnyis po de dag dang lhan cig tu bcom ldan 'das ga la ba der dong nas phyin pa dang /_bcom ldan 'das kyi zhabs la mgo bos phyag 'tshal te lan gsum bskor ba byas nas/_bcom ldan 'das ga la ba de logs 
[\[27b.5\]]su thal mo sbyar ba btud nas/_bcom ldan 'das la tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag gis rnam pa mang pos mngon par bstod do/_/rnam par sgra bsgrags srid pa gsum du grags/_/bsngags pa'i phreng ba 'jig rten kun tu rgyu/_/bdag gis sems can mchog rnams zhing na 
[\[27b.6\]]mthong /_/de dag kun kyang khyed kyi yon tan gsungs/_/'di na yongs su dag pa'i zhing mchis te/_/dri ma ma mchis ngan song gsum spangs pa/_/de 'dra'i zhing rnams de dag rnam spangs nas/_/khyed kyi thugs rjes ngan pa yongs su bzung /_/'jig rten mgon khyod 
[\[27b.7\]]ye shes dman ma mchis/_/ma lus bde bar gshegs dang khyod du mtshungs/_/shin tu rnam dag bsam pa mngon mdzad pa/_/'di 'dra ba yi zhing 'di khyod kyis bzung /_/tshul khrims thams cad rnam dag 'dod spangs nas/_/gang zhig bskal par gzhan na spyod pa 
[\[28a\]]
[\[28a.1\]]bas/_/su dag byams pa'i sems kyis shin tu 'dir/_/skad cig spyod na'ang 'dir ni khyad par 'phags/_/lus dang ngag dang 'on te yid kyis kyang /_/gang la nyes bgyis rnam pa gsum mchis te/_/ngan song gsum du myong bar gang 'gyur 
[\[28a.2\]]ba/_/de ni tshe 'di nyid la 'byung bar 'gyur/_/byang chub sems dpa' 'di na gang mchis pa/_/de dag rtag tu gdung bar mi bgyi ste/_/de yi las gang log ltung mchi ba yang /_/de dag 'dir ni klad pa na bas 'byang /_/'di ni byang chub sems dpa' skyon med grang /_/
[\[28a.3\]]dam pa'i chos gsungs chos mchog 'dzin pa rnams/_/de dag 'di las phyin cad nam du yang /_/dran pa mi 'khrug blo yang nyams mi 'gyur/_/gang zhig bdud kyis bcings pa gcad pa dang /_/de bzhin nyon mongs las bcas spyod 'tshal ba/_/de ni zhing 'dir dam 
[\[28a.4\]]pa'i chos nyid gsungs/_/'di la ye shes ldan pa'i mchog tu 'gyur/_/gang zhig bskal pa bye bar zhing gzhan du/_/sems ldan dam chos 'dzin par gyur pa bas/_/su dag snga dro cig tsam mi mjed du/_/'di ni de bas mchog tu mkhas pas dgongs/_/gang gis mngon 
[\[28a.5\]]par dga' ba'i 'jig rten dang /_/de bzhin tshe dpag med kyi bde can mchog_/de na sdug bsngal sdug bsngal sgra ma mchis/_/der ni yon tan bgyi ba rmad mi che/_/nyon mongs 'byung gnas 'jig rten nyes gang 'dir/_/zhe sdang can gyi rab tu nyes bzod la/_/chos 
[\[28a.6\]]'di la yang gzhan dag 'dzud pa ni/_/'di ni de bas dka' ba'i mchog tu 'gyur/_/snying rjes rlan sems sdug bsngal mang las grol/_/bla med bla ma khyod la bdag phyag 'tshal/_/gtum sems can la chos gang 'chad pa ni/_/bdag po 'jig rten na yang 
[\[28a.7\]]de sbyang dka'/_/shin tu rgya che ba yi 'khor 'di dang /_/phyogs su rnam par grags pa'i sangs rgyas sras/_/chos kyis mi ngoms rgya mtsho 'dre ba tshogs/_/de dag la ni rgyal ba'i byang chub gsungs/_/tshangs pa dbang po 'jig rten skyong ba dang /_/lha klu lha min 
[\[28b\]]
[\[28b.1\]]mi 'am cir bcas pa/_/chos nyid 'tshal ba mang po tshogs pa la/_/ji ltar mos pa bzhin du chos nyid gsungs/_/dge bsnyen dge bsnyen ma dang dge slong dang /_/dge slong ma dang 'khor bzhi 'di dag tshogs/_/ji ltar thos nas nor grub rab 'dong bar/_/de ltar 
[\[28b.2\]]de dag la ni chos bstan gsol/_/gang rnams sangs rgyas theg la rtag mos dang /_/de bzhin rang rgyal theg dang nyan thos theg_/theg pa gang gi sems bsam gang mkhyen pa/_/de la sman pa'i slad du de gsungs shig_/sangs rgyas rigs la gnas phyir 
[\[28b.3\]]gang bzhugs pa/_/dkon mchog gsum po gang las byung ba yi/_/byang chub nges sems dag la sman slad du/_/chos kyi rgyal po khyod la bdag gsol lo/_/khyod kyi bsngags pa'i phreng ba grags thos nas/_/shes rab yangs pa 'di rnams bde bar lhags/_/
[\[28b.4\]]sems can gtso bo de dag mkhyen pa bzhin/_/de dag la ni bla med spyod pa shod/_/'di ltar bde gshegs mkhyen pa bsam mi khyab/_/dgra bcom bdag nyid chen pos 'di min/_/'di ni rang rgyal gyi yang yul ma lags/_/bdag kyang 'dren la dad pa'i rjes 
[\[28b.5\]]su 'brang /_/bdag gis gang khyod don chen 'di dris pa/_/'jig rten rnam 'dren khyod la bdag bzod gsol/_/gang phyir khyod kyi thugs ni skyo mi mnga'/_/de phyir byang chub lam mchog bstan du gsol/_/de nas bcom ldan 'das kyi spyan sngar tshangs pa khyad par 
[\[28b.6\]]sems kyis tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras nas/_bcom ldan 'das la 'di skad ces gsol to/_/bcom ldan 'das ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' dag lhag pa'i bsam pa brtan zhing yid mi skyo ba rnams lags/_bcom ldan 'das ji ltar na byang chub sems 
[\[28b.7\]]dpa' dag mgo gcig tu smra zhing tshig gdung ba ma mchis pa rnams lags/_ji ltar na dge ba'i rtsa ba rnams mngon par 'phags pa rnams lags/_ji ltar na bslad pa ma mchis shing spyod lam yang mi 'khrug pa rnams lags/_ji ltar na chos dkar po rnams rnam par 'phel 
[\[29a\]]
[\[29a.1\]]bar bgyid pa rnams lags/_ji ltar na sa nas sar 'phar bar bgyid pa rnams lags/_ji ltar na sems can yongs su smin par bgyi ba la thabs mkhas pa rnams lags/_ji ltar na sems can rnams la nye bar spyod pa la mkhas pa rnams lags/_
[\[29a.2\]]ji ltar na byang chub kyi sems srung ba rnams lags/_ji ltar na sems rtse gcig cing yid rnam par ma 'dres pa rnams lags/_ji ltar na chos rnams yongs su tshol ba la mkhas pa rnams lags/_ji ltar na ltung ba las 'byung ba la mkhas pa rnams lags/_ji 
[\[29a.3\]]ltar na nyon mongs pa rnams 'dul ba la mkhas pa rnams lags/_ji ltar na 'khor gyi nang du mchi ba la mkhas pa rnams lags/_ji ltar na chos rnam par 'byed pa la mkhas pa rnams lags/_ji ltar na rgyu'i stobs dang ldan zhing dge ba'i rtsa ba chab mi 'tshal ba rnams 
[\[29a.4\]]lags/_ji ltar na sbyin pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa la ma bstan par sbyin pa dang /_de bzhin du shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa'i bar du ma bstan par shes pa rnams lags/_ji ltar na bsam gtan rnams kyi bde ba las ldog pa la mkhas pa rnams lags/_ji ltar 
[\[29a.5\]]na sangs rgyas kyi chos rnams las phyir mi ldog pa rnams lags/_ji ltar na sangs rgyas kyi rigs mi gcod pa rnams lags/_de skad ces gsol pa dang /_bcom ldan 'das kyis tshangs pa khyad par sems la 'di skad ces bka' stsal to/_/tshangs pa legs 
[\[29a.6\]]so legs so/_/yang tshangs pa khyod de bzhin gshegs pa la de lta bu'i don dri bar sems pa legs so/_/tshangs pa de'i phyir shin tu legs par nyon la yid la zung shig dang bshad do/_/bcom ldan 'das legs so zhes gsol nas tshangs pa khyad par sems 
[\[29a.7\]]bcom ldan 'das kyi ltar nyan pa dang /_bcom ldan 'das kyis 'di skad ces bka' stsal to/_/tshangs pa chos bzhi dang ldan na byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po dag lhag pa'i bsam pa brtan zhing yid mi skyo ba rnams yin te/_bzhi gang zhe na/_sems can 
[\[29b\]]
[\[29b.1\]]rnams la snying rje ba dang /_mi skyo ba'i brtson 'grus dang /_'khor ba la rmi lam lta bur mos pa dang /_sangs rgyas kyi ye shes la mi mnyam pa dang mnyam par yongs su 'jal ba ste/_tshangs pa chos bzhi po de dag dang ldan na byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po dag

[\[29b.3\]]cad la gang zag med par mgo gcig tu smra'o/_/byang chub sems dpa' rnams skye gnas thams cad la dga' ba med par mgo gcig tu smra'o/_/byang chub sems dpa' rnams theg pa chen po'i bsngags pa mgo gcig tu smra'o/_/byang chub sems dpa' rnams 
[\[29b.4\]]dge ba dang mi dge ba'i las rnams chud mi za bar mgo gcig tu smra'o/_/tshangs pa de dag ni byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rnams kyi tshig mgo gcig pa bzhi'o/_/tshangs pa chos bzhi po 'di dag ni byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rnams kyi dge 
[\[29b.5\]]ba'i rtsa ba mngon par 'phags par 'gyur ba ste/_bzhi gang zhe na/_tshul khrims dang /_thos pa dang /_gtong ba dang /_'byung ba ste bzhi po de dag go/_/tshangs pa chos bzhi dang ldan na byang chub sems dpa' rnams mi slad cing spyod lam rnam par 'khrug par mi 'gyur te/_
[\[29b.6\]]bzhi gang zhe na/_rnyed pa med pa dang /_smad pa med pa dang /_grags pa med pa dang /_sdug bsngal ba ste/_tshangs pa chos bzhi po de dag dang ldan na byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rnams mi slad cing spyod lam rnam par 'khrug par mi 'gyur ro/_/tshangs 
[\[29b.7\]]pa chos bzhi dang ldan na byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rnams dge ba'i rtsa ba rnams 'phel bar byed de/_bzhi gang zhe na/_sems can rnams byang chub la yang dag par 'dzud pa dang /_sbyin pa byin pa na rnam par smin pa la mi re ba dang /_dam pa'i chos yongs 
[\[30a\]]
[\[30a.1\]]su 'dzin pa dang /_byang chub sems dpa' rnams la shes rab ston pa ste/_bzhi po 'di dag go/_/tshangs pa chos bzhi dang ldan na byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rnams sa nas sar 'phar te/_bzhi gang zhe na/_dge ba'i rtsa ba bsags

[\[30a.5\]]pa ste bzhi po de dag go/_/tshangs pa chos bzhi dang ldan na byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rnams byang chub kyi sems chud mi gzon te bzhi gang zhe na/_sangs rgyas rjes su dran pa yid la byed pa dang /_dge ba'i rtsa ba thams cad la byang chub kyi sems sngon du 'gro 
[\[30a.6\]]ba dang /_dge ba'i bshes gnyen la bsten pa dang /_theg pa chen po la yang dag par bsngags pa ste/_bzhi po 'di dag go/_/tshangs pa chos bzhi dang ldan na byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rnams sems rtse gcig cing yid rnam par ma 'dres pa rnams 
[\[30a.7\]]su rig par bya ste/_bzhi gang zhe na/_nyan thos kyi sems rnam par spong ba dang /_rang sangs rgyas kyi yid la byed pa gtong ba dang /_chos yongs su tshol bas mi ngoms pa dang /_ji ltar thos pa bzhin du chos ston pa ste bzhi po 'di dag go/_/tshangs pa chos bzhi dang 
[\[30b\]]
[\[30b.1\]]ldan na byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rnams chos yongs su tshol te/_bzhi gang zhe na/_rnyed par dka' ba'i phyir rin po cher 'du shes pa dang /_nad thams cad rab tu zhi bar byed pa'i phyir rtsir 'du shes pa dang /_chud mi za ba'i phyir don du 'du shes pa

[\[30b.3\]]nang nas mi skye ba'i chos la bzod pa dang /_mi 'pho bas mi 'gag pa la bzod pa dang /_rgyu la so sor rtog pas rten cing 'brel bar 'byung ba la bzod pa dang /_gcig nas gcig tu sems phan tshun du gyur pa ste bzhi po 'di dag go/_/tshangs pa chos bzhi dang ldan na/_

[\[30b.5\]]go/_/tshangs pa chos bzhi dang ldan na byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rnams 'khor gyi nang du 'gro ste/_bzhi gang zhe na/_chos 'dod pa'i phyir te/_klan ka tshol ba'i bsam pas ma yin pa dang /_ri mo dang bcas pa'i phyir te nga rgyal dang khengs bsam 
[\[30b.6\]]yin pa dang /_dge ba gang yin pa tshol ba'i phyir te bdag spyoms pas ma yin pa dang /_sems can rnams dge ba'i rtsa ba la gzud pa'i phyir te rnyed pa dang /_bkur sti dang /_tshigs su bcad pa'i phyir ma yin pa ste/_bzhi po 'di dag go/_/tshangs pa chos bzhi dang 
[\[30b.7\]]ldan na byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rnams chos kyi sbyin pa rnam par 'byed de/_bzhi gang zhe na/_dam pa'i chos yongs su 'dzin pa dang /_bdag nyid kyi shes rab gsal bar byed pa dang /_skyes bu dam pa'i las byed pa dang /_kun nas nyon mongs pa dang /_
[\[31a\]]
[\[31a.1\]]rnam par byang ba ston pa ste bzhi po 'di dag go/_/tshangs pa chos bzhi dang ldan na byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rnams rgyu'i stobs dang ldan pas dge ba'i rtsa ba chud mi za ba yin te/_bzhi gang zhe na/_gzhan gyi 'khrul pa la nyes par mi lta 
[\[31a.2\]]ba dang /_khros pa rnams la byams pa sgom pa dang /_chos rnams kyi rgyu ston pa dang /_byang chub kyi sems mi gtong ba ste bzhi po 'di dag go/_/tshangs pa chos bzhi dang ldan na byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rnams pha rol tu phyin pa drug la ma bstan par 
[\[31a.3\]]shes pa rnams yin te/_bzhi gang zhe na/_sbyin pa dag la sngon du 'gro ba rnams yin/_ltung ba rnams kyi pha rol la rtsod par mi byed pa yin/_sems can yongs su smin par bya ba'i phyir bsdu ba'i dngos po la mkhas pa yin/_chos rnams la zab mor mos pa rnams yin te bzhi 
[\[31a.4\]]po 'di dag go/_/tshangs pa chos bzhi dang ldan na byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rnams bsam gtan la gnas nas 'dod pa'i khams su skye'o/_/bzhi gang zhe na/_sems las su rung ba dang /_dge ba'i rtsa ba rnams kyis stobs bskyed pa dang /_sems can 
[\[31a.5\]]rnams yongs su mi gtong ba dang thabs dang shes rab sgom pa ste/_bzhi po 'di dag go/_/tshangs pa chos bzhi dang ldan na byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rnams sangs rgyas kyi chos rnams las phyir mi ldog pa yin te/_bzhi gang zhe na/_'khor ba dpag tu med pa 
[\[31a.6\]]yongs su 'dzin pa dang /_sangs rgyas dpag tu med pa la mchod cing rim gror byed pa dang /_byams pa dpag tu med pa sgom pa dang /_snying rje dpag tu med pa sgom pa ste bzhi po 'di dag go/_/tshangs pa chos bzhi dang ldan na byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rnams sangs 
[\[31a.7\]]rgyas kyi rigs mi gcod de/_bzhi gang zhe na/_sngar dam bcas pa las mi 'gyur ba dang /_ji skad smras pa de bzhin byed pa dang /_dge ba'i rtsa ba rnams la shin tu mos pa dang /_bsam pa'i nan tan la gnas pa ste/_tshangs pa chos bzhi po 'di dag dang ldan na byang chub sems 
[\[31b\]]
[\[31b.1\]]dpa' sems dpa' chen po rnams sangs rgyas kyi rigs mi gcod do/_/bcom ldan 'das kyis bzhi pa mngon par bsgrub pa 'di bshad pa na/_lha dang mir bcas pa'i skye dgu rnams las srog chags stong phrag sum cu rtsa gnyis bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu 
[\[31b.2\]]sems skyes so/_/byang chub sems dpa' lnga stong yang mi skye ba'i chos la bzod pa skyes so/_/sangs rgyas kyi zhing sna tshogs nas 'dus pa'i byang chub sems dpa' de dag gis kyang bcom ldan 'das la mchod pa bya ba'i phyir stong gsum gyi stong chen po'i 'jig rten gyi khams 'dir 
[\[31b.3\]]lha'i me tog rnams kyis pus mo nub tsam du khebs par byas so/_/de nas gzhon nur gyur pa dra ba can gyi 'od kyis tshangs pa khyad par sems la 'di skad ces smras so/_/tshangs pa khyod bcom ldan 'das kyis byang chub sems dpa' tshul bzhin dri ba 'dri ba la mkhas pa 
[\[31b.4\]]rnams kyi mchog tu gsungs na/_de ji ltar dris na byang chub sems dpa' tshul bzhin dri ba yin/_khyad par sems kyis smras pa/_rigs kyi bu bdag tu sgro 'dogs pas 'dri na tshul bzhin ma yin pas 'dri'o/_/gzhan du sgro 'dogs pas 'dri na tshul bzhin ma yin par 
[\[31b.5\]]'dri'o/_/chos su sgro 'dogs pas 'dri na tshul bzhin ma yin par 'dri'o/_/dra ba can gyi 'od gang la bdag tu sgro 'dogs pa med pa dang /_gzhan du sgro 'dogs pa med pa dang /_chos su sgro 'dogs pa med pa 'di ni tshul bzhin 'dri'o/_/gzhan yang dra ba can gyi 'od 
[\[31b.6\]]bskyed pa'i phyir 'dri na tshul bzhin ma yin par 'dri'o/_/'gag pa'i phyir 'dri na tshul bzhin ma yin pa'o/_/gnas dang gnas ma yin pa'i phyir 'dri na de ni tshul bzhin ma yin pa'o/_/dra ba can gyi 'od gang la chos gang yang skye ba'i phyir ram/_'gag pa'i phyir ram/_

[\[32a\]]
[\[32a.1\]]yin pa'o/_/'khor ba las shin tu bzla ba'i phyir 'dri na de ni tshul bzhin ma yin pa'o/_/yongs su mya ngan las 'da' ba'i phyir 'dri na de ni tshul bzhin ma yin pa'o/_/dra ba can gyi 'od gang la kun nas nyon mongs pa'i phyir ma yin/_rnam par byang ba'i phyir 
[\[32a.2\]]ma yin/_'khor ba'i phyir ma yin/_'khor ba las shin tu bzla ba'i phyir ma yin/_yongs su mya ngan las 'da' ba'i phyir 'dri ba med pa de ni tshul bzhin 'dri ba'o/_/de ci'i phyir zhe na/_chos mi 'gyur ba nyid ni kun nas nyon mi mongs/_rnam par byang bar mi 'gyur/_mi 'khor zhing 
[\[32a.3\]]yongs su mya ngan las mi 'da'o/_/gzhan yang dra ba can gyi 'od 'thob pa'i phyir 'dri na de ni tshul bzhin ma yin pa'o/_/mngon du bya ba'i phyir 'dri na de ni tshul bzhin ma yin pa'o/_/mngon par shes pa'i phyir 'dri na de ni tshul bzhin ma yin pa'o/_/yongs su shes pa dang 
[\[32a.4\]]sgom pa'i phyir 'dri na de ni tshul bzhin ma yin pa'o/_/'bral ba'i phyir 'dri na de ni tshul bzhin ma yin pa'o/_/dra ba can gyi 'od gang la thogs pa med pa dang /_'dzin pa med pa dang /_mngon par 'du bya ba med pa dang /_kun tu rtog pa med pa dang /_mngon par shes pa med 
[\[32a.5\]]pa dang /_gnas med pa dang /_bsgom pa med pa dang /_bsgom par lta ba med pa de ni tshul bzhin 'dri ba'o/_/gzhan yang dra ba can gyi 'od 'di ni dge ba'o/_/'di ni mi dge ba'o/_/'di ni zag pa dang bcas pa'o/_/'di ni zag pa med pa'o/_/'di ni kha na ma tho ba dang 
[\[32a.6\]]bcas pa'o/_/'di ni kha na ma tho ba med pa'o/_/'di ni 'dus byas so/_/'di ni 'dus ma byas so/_/'di ni 'jig rten pa'o/_/'di ni 'jig rten las 'das pa'o/_/zhes 'dri na de ni tshul bzhin ma yin pa ste/_dra ba can gyi 'od ji tsam du gnyis su 'jug pas 'dri ba 
[\[32a.7\]]de tsam du tshul bzhin ma yin pa'o/_/dra ba can gyi 'od gang la gnyis med pa dang /_gnyis ma yin pa med pa dang /_'du shes med pa dang /_ming med pa dang /_spyi mi 'jug pa de ni tshul bzhin 'dri ba'o/_/gzhan yang dra ba can gyi 'od sangs rgyas tha dad du byed pa dang /_chos tha 
[\[32b\]]
[\[32b.1\]]dad du byed pa dang /_dge 'dun tha dad du byed pa dang /_zhing tha dad du byed pa dang /_sems can tha dad du byed pa dang /_theg pa tha dad du byed pa de dag ni tshul bzhin ma yin pa'o/_/dra ba can gyi 'od gang la chos gang yang tha dad par ram/_gcig par mi 'dri ba de ni tshul bzhin 'dri 
[\[32b.2\]]ba'o/_/gzhan yang dra ba can gyi 'od chos thams cad ni tshul bzhin no/_/chos thams cad ni tshul bzhin ma yin pa'o/_/smras pa tshangs pa ji ltar na chos thams cad ni tshul bzhin/_chos thams cad ni tshul bzhin ma yin/_smras pa/_chos thams cad bsam gyis mi khyab pa'i

[\[34a.1\]]gal te sangs rgyas kyi zhing gang gA'i klung gi bye ma snyed du dong yang bstan pa 'di las mi thar to/_/rigs kyi bu 'di lta ste dper na/_skyes bu byis pa'i rang bzhin can nam mkhas 'jigs shing skrag ste bros kyang de ga las gar song ba de dang de dag tu nam mkha'

[\[37a.7\]{]brdzun [,]rdzun [}]pa'i lta ba'ang mi 'dzin to/_/nam mkha' lta bur rnam dag par/_/gang gi 'jig rten 'di shes pa/_/grags pa chen po'i 'jig rten gyi/_/snang ba lta bur gyur pa yin/_/nga nyid kyis ni mthong ba ltar/_/de ltar gang gis 'jig rten mthong /_/phyogs dang phyogs mtshams 
[\[37b\]]
[\[37b.1\]]gang bzhugs pa/_/rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas de dag gzigs/_/chos rnams ngo bo nyid med par/_/brten pa'i chos rnams rab tu shes/_/gang rnams brten pa shes pa ni/_/de dag chos nyid shes pa yin/_/gang rnams chos nyid rab shes pa/_/de dag stong pa nyid de shes/_/
[\[37b.2\]]gang rnams stong nyid rab shes pa/_/de yi 'dren pa de rnams mthong /_/gang rnams 'jig rten bstan pa 'di/_/rna lam grags par 'gyur ba ni/_/de dag 'jig rten spyod mod kyi/_/'jig rten 'di la gnas ma yin/_/lta la gnas pa gang su dag_/gzhan gyi sa ni 'di ma yin/_/
[\[37b.3\]]'jig rten du ni spyod byed kyang /_/'jig rten 'di la gnas pa med/_/sangs rgyas mya ngan 'das nas kyang /_/gang rnams bzod pa 'dir mos pa/_/de bzhin gshegs pa chos kyi sku/_/rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas de la bzhugs/_/gang gis tshul 'di shes pa ni/_/des ni nga yi chos 
[\[37b.4\]]bzung ste/_/de yis nga la rtag tu mchod/_/de dag 'jig rten ston pa'o/_/gang zhig 'jig rten bstan pa 'di/_/rna lam grags par gyur pa ni/_/sdig can bdud kyis de dag la/_/glags kyang rnyed par mi 'gyur ro/_/gang gis rnam pa 'di shes pa/_/de dag phyug cing longs spyod che/_/
[\[37b.5\]]de dag sbyin la rtag par dga'/_/de dag tshul khrims phun sum tshogs/_/gang gis 'jig rten 'di shes pa/_/de dag bzod pa'i stobs ldan pa/_/de dag brtson 'grus rtogs par rigs/_/mkhas pa de dag bsam gtan dga'/_/gang gis rnam pa 'di shes pa/_/de dag ting 'dzin 
[\[37b.6\]]phun sum tshogs/_/de dag nye bar bzhi la dga'/_/bstan pa de dag shes rab 'phags/_/gang na 'di 'dra grags pa yi/_/phyogs de sangs rgyas stongs ma yin/_/sems dpa' chen po ring mi thogs/_/byang chub snying por 'gro bar 'gyur/_/gang rnams 'jig rten bstan pa 'di/_/
[\[37b.7\]]rna lam grags par 'gyur ba dag_/g.yon can bdud rnams bcom nas ni/_/byang chub mchog tu rdzogs 'tshang rgya/_/de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis tshangs pa khyad par sems la 'di skad ces bka' stsal to/_/tshangs pa de bzhin gshegs pa 'jig rten gyi yul las 'das pas 
[\[38a\]]
[\[38a.1\]]'jig rten dang /_'jig rten kun 'byung ba dang /_'jig rten 'gog pa dang /_'jig rten 'gog par 'gro ba'i lam du yang 'dogs so/_/tshangs pa de la 'jig rten zhes bya ba ni phung po lnga rnams so/_/gang de la chags pa de ni 'jig rten kun 'byung ba'o/_/gang phung

[\[38a.7\]]pa rnams kyi bden pa yin du zin na/_de ni byol song ba lang dang /_bong bu dang /_sems can dmyal ba pa thams cad kyi yang 'phags pa'i bden par 'gyur ro/_/de ci'i phyir zhe na/_'di ltar de dag ni sdug bsngal ba'i tshor ba rnams myong ngo /_/tshangs pa 'di ni sdug bsngal kun

[\[38b.1\]]'byung ba zhes bya ba de ji ste/_'phags pa rnams kyi bden pa yin du zin na de ni gang 'gro ba rnams su 'byung ba'i 'gro ba thams cad du skyes pa'i sems can rnams kyi yang 'phags pa'i bden par 'gyur ro/_/'di ni 'gog pa'o zhes bya ba de ji ste 'phags pa'i bden pa yin du zin na/_

[\[39b.1\]]rnams dngos po 'jig pas 'gog pa 'phags pa'i bden pa'o zhes smra bar 'gyur ba de dag ni lam log par zhugs pa yin te/_nga yang de dag gi ston pa ma yin/_de dag kyang nga'i nyan thos ma yin no/_/tshangs pa ltos shig nga byang /_/chub kyi snying por 'dug nas bden pa'am/[ {]brdzun [,]rdzun [}]pa zhes

[\[40a\]]
[\[40a.1\]]shin tu rnam par mi dmigs pa de ltar mkhyen to/_/de ci'i phyir zhe na/_tshangs pa ngas 'di ltar chos gang yang mngon par sangs rgyas pa de ni ma mthong /_ma thos/_mi dran/_ma rtogs/_rnam par mi rig cing /_gzung ba yang med/_sbyar ba yang med/_brjod du yang

[\[40a.7\]]'di la ni dge ba yang ma mchis/_mi dge ba yang ma mchis so/_/'jig rten ni bde ba la mngon par chags na/_'di la ni bde ba yang ma mchis/_sdug bsngal ba yang ma mchis so/_/'jig rten ni sangs rgyas 'byung ba la mngon par chags na 'di la sangs

[\[42a.1\]]de dag blos mnyam pa ma mchis par 'gyur ro/_/de dag thos pas mi mnyam pa dang mnyam par 'gyur ro/_/de dag gzhal ba las 'das pa'i slad du mtshungs pa ma mchis par 'gyur ro/_/de dag spobs pas thogs pa ma mchis par 'gyur ro/_/de dag gzungs

[\[42b.1\]]de dag gi bzod pa gsum yongs su rdzogs par bgyis so/_/de dag byang chub kyi snying po yongs su rdzogs pa la gnas so/_/de dag gis bdud kyi sde rab tu bcom mo/_/de dag thams cad mkhyen pa nyid thob bo/_/de dag chos kyi 'khor lo rab tu skor bar 'gyur ro/_/de dag

[\[52a.1\]]sems dpa' bzhi byung ste/_bzhi gang zhe na/_'di lta ste/_byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po smon lam yang dag 'phags zhes bya ba dang /_khyad par bzang zhes bya ba dang /_ye shes zla ba zhes bya ba dang /_byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' 
[\[52a.2\]]chen po gzhan gyis mi thub rgyal mtshan zhes bya ba ste/_de dag thal mo sbyar nas gang la phyag bya ba'i de bzhin gshegs pa yang dag pa 'di'i nang na gang yin snyam ste/_phyag 'tshal zhing 'khod pa dang /_de dag gi bar snang las 'di ltar 'khor 'di thams cad 'di lta ste/_de bzhin 
[\[52a.3\]]gshegs pa'i kha dog tu kha dog gcig tu snang ba ni gzhon nur gyur pa dra ba can gyi 'od kyi bye brag go zhes sgra thos so/_/de nas byang chub sems dpa' de bzhis 'di skad ces 'dzer te/_bden pa dang bden pa'i tshig gang gis ji ltar 'khor 'di kha dog dang gzugs gcig ste 
[\[52a.4\]]bye brag med par de bzhin du chos thams cad kyang bye brag med ce na/_bden pa dang bden pa'i tshig des bcom ldan 'das shAkya thub pa la tha dad du dbye ba lhag pa gang mthong bas de bzhin gshegs pa la mchod pa byed par 'gyur ba 'ga' zhig mthong bar shog shig ces 
[\[52a.5\]]byas pa dang /_de nas de'i mod la bcom ldan 'das seng ge'i khri pad ma can dang bcas te/_ta la gang tsam du mngon par 'phags par gyur to/_/de nas bcom ldan 'das la byang chub sems dpa' bzhi po de dag gi mgo bos phyag 'tshal nas tshig 'di skad ces 
[\[52a.6\]]'dzer to/_/bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa rnams kyi de bzhin gshegs pa'i ye shes ni bsam gyis mi khyab bo/_/'di ltar sems can rnams la bde ba bstan pas byang chub sems dpa' dra ba can gyi 'od kyi bsod nams dang smon lam 'di'ang bsam gyis mi 
[\[52a.7\]]khyab bo/_/de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis gzhon nur gyur pa dra ba can gyi 'od la 'di skad ces bka' stsal to/_/rigs kyi bu khyod kyis sangs rgyas kyi bya ba byas te/_sems can tshad med pa rnams khyod kyis byang chub la bkod kyis/_rdzu 'phrul 'di phyir rtul cig_/
[\[52b\]]
[\[52b.1\]]de nas gzhon nur gyur pa dra ba can gyi 'od kyis 'od de dag phyir brtul to/_/'od de'i phyir brtul ma thag tu 'khor de thams cad spyod lam snga ma ji lta ba bzhin du snang ngo /_/bcom ldan 'das kyang snga ma kho na bzhin du seng ge'i khri pad mo can la bzhugs par snang ngo /_/

[\[52b.3\]]pa 'jig rten gyi khams de ni rin po che thams cad ston pa zhes bya'o/_/smras pa de na de bzhin gshegs pa'i mtshan ci zhes bya ba zhig chos 'chad/_smras pa/_de bzhin gshegs pa de'i mtshan rin po che gdugs gcig pa zhes bya'o/_/smras pa/_'jig rten gyi khams de 'di 
[\[52b.4\]]nas ji srid cig yod/_smras pa/_bcom ldan 'das mkhyen pas mkhyen to/_/smras pa/_khyed rnams 'dir ci la 'ongs/_smras pa/_byang chub sems dpa' dra ba can gyi 'od kyis 'od btang nas 'od des kho bo cag la reg ste/_reg ma thag tu bcom ldan 'das shAkya thub pa'i 
[\[52b.5\]]mtshan dang /_byang chub sems dpa' dra ba can gyi 'od kyi ming thos nas/_bcom ldan 'das dang skyes bu dam pa 'di la blta ba dang /_phyag bya ba dang /_bsnyen bkur bya ba'i phyir bdag cag 'dir 'ongs so/_/de nas bcom ldan 'das la gnas brtan 'od srung chen pos 
[\[52b.6\]]'di skad ces gsol to/_/bcom ldan 'das 'jig rten gyi khams rin po che thams cad ston pa zhes bgyi ba bcom ldan 'das rin po che gdugs gcig pa de bzhin gshegs pa'i sangs rgyas kyi zhing de ji srid cig na mchis/_bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/_

[\[53a\]]
[\[53a.1\]]de bzhin gshegs pa'i sangs rgyas kyi zhing yod do/_/gsol pa de dag de nas ji srid kyis 'dir mchis/_bka' stsal pa/_sems kyi skad cig pa gcig gis der mi snang bar gyur te 'dir phyin to/_/gsol pa/_bcom ldan 'das 'di ltar byang chub sems 
[\[53a.2\]]dpa' dra ba can gyi 'od kyis 'od zer ji srid du mchis pa dang /_rigs kyi bu 'di dag da ltar myur du phyin te/_byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi 'od zer gtong ba dang /_rdzu 'phrul gyi stobs ni ngo mtshar to/_/bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/_'od srung de de bzhin 
[\[53a.3\]]te/_ji skad zer ba de bzhin no/_/gang nyan thos dang /_rang sangs rgyas thams cad kyis ma yin pa byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi spyod pa ni bsam gyis mi khyab bo/_/de nas tshe dang ldan pa 'od srung chen pos gzhon nur gyur pa dra ba can gyi 'od la 'di skad ces smras so/_/rigs 
[\[53a.4\]]kyi bu khyod kyi 'od kyis 'khor 'di dag la reg nas kha dog gcig tu 'di lta ste/_gser gyi kha dog tu gyur na 'di gang gi mthu/_smras pa/_bcom ldan 'das nyid la 'od srung chen po khyod kyis gsol cig dang de nyid khyod la ston to/_/de nas bcom ldan 'das la tshe dang 
[\[53a.5\]]ldan pa 'od srung chen pos 'di skad ces gsol to/_/bcom ldan 'das ltas sngar byung ba 'di su'i lags/_bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/_'od srung dra ba can gyi 'od byang chub thob pa'i 'khor kha dog gcig tu 'di lta ste/_gser gyi kha dog tu 'gyur

[\[53a.7\]]srung chen pos gsol pa/_bcom ldan 'das byang chub sems dpa' gang sangs rgyas kyi zhing der skye bar 'gyur ba de ni de bzhin gshegs pa nyid du gzung bar bgyi'o/_/bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/_'od srung ji skad zer ba de bzhin te/_de ni de bzhin

[\[53b.1\]]gshegs pa nyid du rig par bya'o/_/de nas 'khor de'i nang nas srog chags stong phrag bzhi bcu rtsa bzhi bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu sems bskyed nas sangs rgyas kyi zhing der smon lam 'debs shing 'di skad du/_bcom ldan 'das nam

[\[53b.5\]]tsam gyis bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu mngon par rdzogs par 'tshang rgya bar 'gyur/_dra ba can gyi 'od kyis smras pa/_btsun pa 'od srung chen po la la zhig gis sgyu ma sprul pa'i skyes bu la rigs kyi bu khyod yun ji tsam gyis bla na med pa yang dag par 
[\[53b.6\]]rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu mngon par rdzogs par 'tshang rgya bar 'gyur zhes dris na ji skad du lung ston/_smras pa rigs kyi bu sgyu mas sprul pa la yang dag par yongs su grub pa med na de ci zhig lung ston par 'gyur/_smras pa/_btsun pa 'od srung chen po de de

[\[54a.1\]]zhing 'gyur ba med pa/_yongs su rtogs pa med pa/_byed pa med pa de bzhin du khyod kyang gal te de 'dra na khyod tshad med pa'i sems can rnams kyi don ji ltar byed/_smras pa/_btsun pa 'od srung chen po sgyu ma'i rang bzhin gang yin pa de byang chub

[\[55a.1\]]med la/_zad pa'am/_med par gyur pa yang ma yin na ci khyed kyi 'dod chags dang /_zhe sdang dang /_gti mug rnams 'dug gam/_smras pa/_rigs kyi bu byis pa so so'i skye bo phyin ci log dang ldan pa rnams kyi rtog pa dang /_rnam par rtog pa dang

[\[56a.1\]]bdud kyi rigs kyi lha ji snyed pa yod pa de dag bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu nges par 'gyur ro/_/sbyin pa dang gtong ba rgya cher 'gyur ro/_/'od srung 'jig rten gyi khams de lag mthil ltar mnyam par 'gyur ro/_/gos ka tsa

[\[56a.3\]]ro/_/rin po che'i pad ma chen po dri dang ldan pa dag kyang 'byung bar 'gyur ro/_/'jig rten gyi khams de dri ma med cing rgya che bar 'gyur ro/_/yang 'od srung 'jig rten gyi khams khyad par gyi yon tan bsdus pa de na bcom ldan 'das kun tu 'od rnam par 'phrul pa 'od zer gyi 
[\[56a.4\]]rgyal po de bzhin gshegs pa de'i byang chub sems dpa'i dge 'dun mtha' yas pa bsgrub pa'i chos bgro ba la mkhas pa/_rdzu 'phrul gyis rnam par 'phrul pa 'od kyis kun tu brgyan pa/_gzungs kyi gter thob pa/_ting nge 'dzin thob pa/_thogs pa med pa'i 
[\[56a.5\]]spobs pa dang /_ye shes kyis ston pa la mkhas pa/_mngon par shes pa dang /_rig pa dang /_mi 'jigs pa thob pa/_bdud dang phyir rgol ba rnams bcom pa/_dran pa dang /_blo gros dang /_rtog pa dang /_ngo tsha shes pa dang /_khrel yod pa dang /_shes rab sbyangs 
[\[56a.6\]]pa'i ye shes kyis yongs su sgom pa dpag tu med par 'gyur ro/_/yang 'od srung sangs rgyas kyi zhing de na bud med skye bar mi 'gyur te/_byang chub sems dpa' de dag thams cad pad ma la skyil mo krung bcas shing 'dug par brdzus te skye bar 'gyur ro/_/byang chub

[\[66a.4\]]du na log par nges pa'i sems can rnams kyi phyir byang chub tu smon lam 'debs te/_de'i phyir byang chub sems dpa' zhes bya'o/_/de bas na rigs kyi bu byang chub sems dpa' log par nges pa'i sems can rnams la snying rje bar byas nas byang chub tu smon lam 'debs te de'i phyir

[\[80b.1\]]dag ni spangs pa yin/_/de dag nyon mongs rgyu rnams kyang /_/sdig pa med par dag par shes/_/rkyen la lhag par mos pa la/_/lta ba dag ni yod ma yin/_/'di ltar chos rnams thams cad ni/_/rkyen las rag lus dngos po med/_/bdag tu lta dang sangs rgyas lta/_/stong par lta ba

[\[85a\]]
[\[85a.1\]]pa la ma yin/_kha na ma tho ba med pa la ma yin/_'dus byas la ma yin/_'dus ma byas la ma yin/_'khor ba la ma yin/_mya ngan las 'das pa la mi 'jug pa 'di ni chos kyi nan tan zhes bya ste/_chos thams cad la nan tan du bya ba med pa 'di ni chos kyi nan tan no/_/
[\[85a.2\]]chos su 'du shes pa la chos kyi nan tan med de/_chos 'di la nan tan bya'o zhes spro bar byed/_nan tan du mi bya'o zhes spro bar byed na ma zhugs pa'o/_/chos thams cad kyi rjes su zhugs pa'i nan tan 'di ni chos kyi nan tan no/_/spros pa med pas gnyen po med pa 'di ni chos kyi nan tan 
[\[85a.3\]]zhes bya'o/_/de nas bcom ldan 'das la lha'i bu phyir mi ldog pas 'di skad ces gsol to/_/bcom ldan 'das gang yang dag pa'i nan tan 'di la zhugs pa dang /_'jug par 'gyur ba de dag la shin tu log pa'i nan tan ma mchis so/_/de ci'i slad du zhe na/_bcom ldan 'das 
[\[85a.4\]]shin tu yang dag pa'i nan tan 'di lags te/_log pa'i lam du zhugs pa rnams la nan tan gang yang ma mchis so/_/yang dag pa'i lam la gnas pa rnams la chos kyi nan tan ma mchis so/_/yang dag par zhugs pa rnams la ni mi mnyam pa'i chos gang yang ma mchis so/_/de ci'i 
[\[85a.5\]]slad du zhe na/_bcom ldan 'das chos thams cad ni mnyam pa khyad par ma mchis pa'o/_/de nas tshangs pa khyad par sems kyis lha'i bu de la 'di skad ces smras so/_/lha'i bu khyod nan tan 'di la zhugs sam/_smras pa/_tshangs pa gal te bcom ldan 'das kyis nan tan la gnyis su 
[\[85a.6\]]bshad par gyur na bdag kyang nan tan 'di la nan tan du byed do/_/nan tan ni gnyis su med pa ste/_de la gang nan tan byed pa dang /_gang la nan tan byed pa de yang med do/_/gang yang nan tan du byed pa de med de/_'on kyang tshangs pa bdag ni lhag par mi byed pa dang sgro mi 'dogs pa'i 
[\[85a.7\]]phyir nan tan 'di la zhugs so/_/chos nyid ji lta bar chos kyi nan tan yang de bzhin te/_de ni de bzhin nyid la de ltar zhugs na zhugs pa zhes bya'o/_/tshangs pas smras pa/_lha'i bu khyod sangs rgyas kyi zhing 'di ni sngon ngas ma mthong ngo /_/smras pa/_tshangs pa sangs rgyas kyi zhing 'dis kyang 
[\[85b\]]
[\[85b.1\]]sngon bdag ma mthong ngo /_/smras pa/_sangs rgyas kyi zhing 'di ni blta'o zhe'am/_mi lta'o zhes rtog pa'am/_rnam par rtog pa med do/_/smras pa/_tshangs pa bdag sngon mthong ngo zhe'am/_sngon ma mthong ngo zhes bdag kyang mi rtog rnam par mi rtog go/_/smras pa/_lha'i bu sngon ma 
[\[85b.2\]]mthong ba mthong ba su smras pa/_byis pa so so'i skye bo thams cad kyis ni 'phags pa'i nges par 'jug pa sngon ma mthong ngo /_/tshangs pa nam yang dag par nges par zhugs pa de ni sngon ma mthong ba mthong ngo /_/nges pa de ni mig gi rnam par shes pas shes pa ma yin/_rna ba dang /_sna 
[\[85b.3\]]dang /_lce dang /_lus dang /_yid kyi rnam par shes pas shes par bya ba ma yin te/_de bzhin nyid ji lta ba de bzhin du blta bar bya'o/_/mig gi de bzhin nyid ji lta ba dang /_rna ba'i de bzhin nyid ji lta ba dang /_sna'i de bzhin nyid ji lta ba dang /_lce'i de bzhin nyid ji lta ba dang /_lus kyi de 
[\[85b.4\]]bzhin nyid ji lta ba dang /_yid kyi de bzhin nyid ji lta ba de bzhin du blta ste/_gang de ltar mthong ba de dag ni yang dag par mthong ba'o/_/de nas bcom ldan 'das la lha'i dbang po brgya byin gyis 'di skad ces gsol to/_/bcom ldan 'das ded dpon nor bu rin po che'i gling du phyin 
[\[85b.5\]]pa'i mig sngar ni nor bu rin po che 'ba' zhig gda'o/_/de bzhin du bcom ldan 'das skyes bu dam pa bsam gyis mi khyab pa'i chos rin po che dang ldan pa rnams ci dang ci spobs pa de dag gis de thams cad chos rin po che nyid du spobs so/_/de nyid yang dag pa'i mtha' yongs su gsal 
[\[85b.6\]]bar bgyid par spobs so/_/'di ltar de dag la bdag tu mngon par chags pa ma mchis/_chos su mngon par chags pa ma mchis/_sems can du mngon par chags pa ma mchis pa'i slad du'o/_/de'i slad du ji ltar ma log pa dang phyin ci ma log par spobs pa de de ltar spobs 
[\[85b.7\]]so/_/ji ltar sngon gyi mtha' yang spyod pa dang /_phyi ma'i mtha' yang mi dmigs pa dang /_da ltar byung ba la yang de lta ba ma mchis pa de ltar spobs so/_/ji ltar ma mos pa rnams kyang mos par bgyid la mos pa rnams kyang rnam par 'grol ba de de ltar spobs so/_/mngon pa'i 
[\[86a\]]
[\[86a.1\]]nga rgyal can rnams kyang tshar bcad par 'gyur la/_nga rgyal ma mchis pa kun 'tshal ba lan ldon pa de ltar spobs so/_/ji ltar bdud kyis glags mi rnyed pa de ltar spobs so/_/gang de ltar nyan pa de dag kyang bdud kyi las las 'das par 'gyur ro/_/ji ltar dge 
[\[86a.2\]]ba'i rtsa ba ma bskyed pa rnams bskyed pa dang /_dge ba'i rtsa ba skyes pa rnams chab mi 'tshal ba dang /_nyon mongs pa skyes pa rnams spong ba dang /_ma skyes pa rnams mi skyed pa de ltar spobs so/_/ji ltar byang chub sems dpa' go cha ma bgos pa rnams go cha 'tshal ba dang /_go cha 
[\[86a.3\]]bgos pa rnams mi ldog par 'gyur ba de ltar spobs so/_/ji ltar dam pa'i chos mi 'chad cing dam pa'i chos yongs su gzung bar bgyid pa de ltar spobs so/_/ji ltar sangs rgyas kyi chos thams cad yongs su rdzogs par 'gyur ba de ltar spobs so/_/bcom ldan 'das de lta 
[\[86a.4\]]bu'i spobs pas ni mu stegs kyi gnas thams cad gzhom par nus so/_/de ci'i slad du zhe na/_bcom ldan 'das wa ni seng ge'i sgra bzod par yang mi nus na rab tu sgrog par lta ga la nus te/_bcom ldan 'das de bzhin du pha rol rgol ba thams cad kyis bla na med pa seng ge'i 
[\[86a.5\]]sgra bzod par mi nus so/_/de nas lha'i bu phyir mi ldog pas lha'i dbang po brgya byin la 'di skad ces smras so/_/kau shi ka seng ge'i sgra seng ge'i sgra zhes bya ba ji tsam gyis na seng ge'i sgra zhes bya/_smras pa/_lha'i bu chos gang la yang mngon par ma chags par brjod pa de dag seng ge'i sgra 
[\[86a.6\]]yin no/_/gang de lta bu la mngon par chags par brjod pa de dag ni seng ge'i sgra ma yin te wa'i sgra'o/_/gang lta ba kun tu slong ba ston pa de dag la seng ge'i sgra med do/_/lha'i dbang po brgya byin gyis smras pa/_lha'i bu khyod seng ge'i sgra ji lta bu yin pa spobs par gyis shig_/smras 
[\[86a.7\]]pa/_kau shi ka gang de bzhin gshegs pa la yang mngon par ma chags par ston na chos ngan pa gzhan lta ci smos te/_de'i phyir seng ge'i sgra zhes bya'o/_/yang dag pa'i nan tan ni seng ge'i sgra ste/_de'i phyir seng ge'i sgra zhes bya'o/_/'di ni mgo gcig tu bstan pa yin te/_de'i phyir 
[\[86b\]]
[\[86b.1\]]seng ge'i sgra zhes bya'o/_/'di ni mi skrag par bstan pa ste/_de'i phyir seng ge'i sgra zhes bya'o/_/kau shi ka gang chos thams cad mi skye ba dang /_mi 'gog pa dang /_mi 'thob par sgom pa'i phyir chos ston pa de dag gi seng ge'i sgra yin te/_gang kun nas nyon mongs pa dang /_rnam par byang 
[\[86b.2\]]ba dang /_kun tu sbyor ba dang 'bral bar bya ba'i phyir chos ston pa de dag la seng ge'i sgra med do/_/kau shi ka seng ge'i sgra zhes bya ba ni 'di lta ste/_mgo gcig tu bstan pa/_chos thams cad gang zag med pa/_bdag med pa yin no/_/kau shi ka seng ge'i sgra seng ge'i sgra zhes bya ba ni 'di lta 
[\[86b.3\]]ste/_mgo gcig tu chos thams cad stong pa nyid dang /_mtshan ma med pa dang /_smon pa med pa yongs su ston pa ni seng ge'i sgra'o/_/dam pa'i chos bsrung ba'i phyir tshig smra ba ni seng ge'i sgra'o/_/'di lta ste/_sems can thams cad rab tu thar bar bya ba'i phyir sangs rgyas su 
[\[86b.4\]]gyur cig ces byang chub tu sems bskyed pa'i tshig smra ba ni seng ge'i sgra'o/_/longs spyod rung ba la chog shes par smra ba ni seng ge'i sgra'o/_/kau shi ka seng ge'i sgra seng ge'i sgra zhes bya ba ni 'di lta ste/_dgon par gnas pa dang /_yang dag par len pa dang /_gtong ba sngon du 'gro ba 
[\[86b.5\]]ni seng ge'i sgra'o/_/tshul khrims yang dag par blangs pa mi 'dor ba ni seng ge'i sgra'o/_/mdza' ba dang mi mdza' ba la sems mnyam pa ni seng ge'i sgra'o/_/brtson pa mi 'dor ba ni seng ge'i sgra'o/_/nyon mongs pa spong ba ni seng ge'i sgra'o/_/shes rab kyis shin tu brtags pa ni 
[\[86b.6\]]seng ge'i sgra'o/_/seng ge'i sgra bstan pa 'di bshad pa'i tshe stong gsum gyi stong chen po'i 'jig rten gyi khams rnam pa drug tu g.yos so/_/sil snyan brgya stong du ma ma brdungs par sgra 'byin to/_/'jig rten rnams snang ba chen pos khyab par gyur to/_/lha'i bu brgya stong a la la zhes 
[\[86b.7\]]sgra 'byin cing tshig 'di skad ces smras so/_/e ma'o deng 'dir lha'i bus seng ge'i sgra 'di bsgrags pas 'dzam bu gling du chos kyi 'khor lo bskor ba lan gnyis byung ngo /_/de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis de'i tshe 'dzum pa mdzad pa dang /_chos nyid kyis 'di ltar 'gyur te/_nam sangs rgyas 
[\[87a\]]
[\[87a.1\]]bcom ldan 'das 'dzum pa mdzad pa de'i tshe 'od zer kha dog sna tshogs 'di lta ste/_mthing ga dang /_ser po dang /_dmar po dang /_dkar po dang /_le brgan dang /_shel dang /_dngul gyi kha dog lta bu zhal nas byung ste/_'jig rten gyi khams mtha' yas pa khyab par byas 
[\[87a.2\]]nas tshangs pa'i 'jig rten gyi bar du mngon par 'phags te phyir log nas/_bcom ldan 'das la lan gsum bskor ba byas nas bcom ldan 'das kyi spyi bor mi snang bar gyur to//_//bam po drug pa ste tha ma/_de nas bcom ldan 'das la tshangs pa khyad par sems kyis 
[\[87a.3\]]tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag gis mngon par bstod do/_/ye shes kun mchog pha rol phyin cing thub pa kun mkhyen kun zil gnon/_/'di la dus gsum sems can spyod pa sna tshogs ci mchis khyod kyis mkhyen/_/gang de shin tu grol ba'i blo yul gang yang de dag bsam pa rnams/_/
[\[87a.4\]]lha yi gtso bo kun mkhyen 'dzum mdzad 'di yi rgyu ni lung bstan gsol/_/nyan thos rang rgyal dgra bcom dri med de dag gis yang spyod yul min/_/sangs rgyas ye shes bsam mi khyab cing mtha' bral khyod la chags pa med/_/khyod kyis sems can spyod pa sems can gang la ci 
[\[87a.5\]]'dra'i gtam brjod mkhyen/_/smra mchog thub dka' tshig dbang 'dzum pa mdzad pa'i rgyu ni bstan par gsol/_/'od 'di shin tu yid 'ong sdug pa dri med rab tu rnam par mdzes/_/nyi zla'i 'od dang de bzhin tshangs pa dbang po'i gzi yang zil gyis gnon/_/de bzhin khor yug khrag khrig 
[\[87a.6\]]ri rab dag kyang shin tu snang bgyis na/_/ci slad khyod kyis 'dzum mdzad de yi rgyu ni rab tu bstan par gsol/_/'dod chags zhe sdang dregs la sogs pa zug rngu bral zhing rab tu zhi/_/'jig rten lha mi lto 'phye'i tshogs rnams byams pas bdag po khyod zhal lta/_/bde gshegs 
[\[87a.7\]]bltas kyang mi ngoms lus ni rab tu sim zhing bde bar 'gyur/_/gang gi slad du 'dzum pa 'di bstan thub pa bdag cag rnams la gsungs/_/khyod kyis chos rnams nam mkha' lta bur rtag tu byed med gsog nyid dang /_/glog dang sprin 'dra sgyu ma lta bur gsob nyid dbu ba lta bu dang /_/rmi 
[\[87b\]]
[\[87b.1\]]lam mthong dang char pa stong 'dra nam mkha' chu zla lta bur mkhyen/_/dge ba 'dzum mdzad 'di rgyu rnar snyan gsung ni rab tu brjod par mdzod/_/'dzin dang rtog dang rnam rtog kun dang bral bas stong pa nyid la mos/_/bdag po shes bya kun la rtog bral rtag tu mtshan ma med la dgyes/_/khyod ni 
[\[87b.2\]]srid pa gsum 'dir gang la'ang mi smon bsam gtan ting 'dzin dgyes/_/gang gi slad du 'dzum pa bstan mdzad 'dren pa 'di ni lung bstan gsol/_/skad la mi chags sgra dang dbyangs dang yi ge dag la'ang de bzhin te/_/'jig rten du yang chos ston blo gtsang bdag dang chos la'ang 
[\[87b.3\]]gnas mi mdzad/_/'dus pa so sor bde gshegs bdag la tha dad chos ston snyam du 'dzin/_/ye shes rdzu 'phrul dbang po stobs mchog rgyas pa shes rab mtshungs med gsungs/_/khyod ni sdug bsngal kun sel na rga 'chi 'joms sman pa'i mchog lags te/_/khyod ni dpa' bo 
[\[87b.4\]]bdud kyi stobs 'joms mthu bo che yi mthu dang ldan/_/khyod ni skyob pa dpung gnyen 'jig rten mgon skyabs sgron ma snang mdzad pa/_/lha min lha dang klus mchod 'dzum pa mdzad pa'i rgyu gang 'dir bstan gsol/_/de skad ces gsol pa dang /_bcom ldan 'das kyis tshangs pa khyad par 
[\[87b.5\]]sems la 'di skad ces bka' stsal to/_/tshangs pa khyod kyis phyir mi ldog pa'i lha'i bu 'di mthong ngam/_/gsol pa/_bcom ldan 'das mthong ngo /_/bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/_tshangs pa phyir mi ldog pa lha'i bu 'di bskal pa brgya stong phrag sum cu rtsa gnyis na 'jig rten 
[\[87b.6\]]gyi khams legs par sprul pa zhes bya bar bskal pa tshangs pas bstod pa la bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu mngon par rdzogs par 'tshang rgya bar 'gyur te/_de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas rig pa dang /_zhabs su ldan pa/_bde 
[\[87b.7\]]bar gshegs pa/_'jig rten mkhyen pa/_bla na med pa/_skyes bu 'dul ba'i kha lo sgyur ba/_lha dang mi rnams kyi ston pa sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das ri rab sgron ma'i rgyal po zhes bya bar 'jig rten du 'byung bar 'gyur te/_de'i sangs rgyas kyi zhing de yang bai dUr+ya dang /_'dzam

[\[88a.1\]]bu chu bo'i gser dang /_rin po che gnyis kyi gzhir 'gyur ro/_/de'i byang chub sems dpa' dge 'dun 'ba' zhig 'byung ste bdud dang phyir rgol ba bcom zhing dpa' bo sha stag go/_/sangs rgyas kyi zhing de na bza' ba dang /_btung ba yang bsam pa tsam gyis 'byor cing 
[\[88a.2\]]dga' ldan gyi longs spyod dang 'dra bar 'gyur ro/_/de bzhin gshegs pa de tshe dpag tu med cing ltung ba med pa'i chos ston par 'gyur/_khyad par sems kyis smras pa/_lha'i bu khyod de bzhin gshegs pas lung bstan to/_/smras pa/_tshangs pa bdag ni de bzhin nyid dang /_

[\[88a.5\]]pa'o/_/smras pa/_lha'i bu de ltar gang la gnas pa med pa de ni tshangs par spyad pa la gnas pa yin/_smras pa/_tshangs pa gang khams gsum la mi gnas pa de ni tshangs par spyad pa la gnas pa'o/_/tshangs pa gang yang khams gsum la gnas pa de ni tshangs par spyad pa la

[\[88b.1\]]pa yin/_smras pa/_tshangs pa lam la rab tu gnas pa zhes bya ba ni chos thams cad la rab tu gnas pa med pa'i tshig [{]bla dgas [,]bla dwags [}]so/_/de ci'i phyir zhe na/_rab tu gnas pa med pa dang /_len pa med pa de ni lam la rab tu gnas pa'o/_/smras pa/_lha'i bu ji ltar bsgom pa na lam

[\[88b.6\]]bzhag pa med pa 'di ni brtson 'grus rtsom pa'o/_/tshangs pa gang la lus dang /_ngag dang yid kyi rtsom pa med pa 'di ni brtson 'grus rtsom pa'o/_/de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis lha'i bu phyir mi ldog pa la legs so zhes bya ba byin nas tshangs pa khyad par sems la bka' stsal 
[\[88b.7\]]pa/_tshangs pa lha'i bu 'di ji skad ston pa de de bzhin no/_/'di ni de'i brtson 'grus rtsom pa'i mchog ste/_gang lus kyi rtsom pa yang med/_ngag gi rtsom pa med/_yid kyi rtsom pa yang med pa 'di ni brtson 'grus rtsom pa'i mchog go/_/tshangs pa ngas mngon par shes pa 
[\[89a\]]
[\[89a.1\]]'das pa'i dus na sbyangs pa'i yon tan yo byad bsnyungs pa thams cad yang dag par blang ba'i brtson 'grus brtsams kyang de bzhin gshegs pa de dag gis bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu lung ma bstan to/_/de bzhin du bla ma rnams la ldang ba dang /_

[\[89a.3\]]phyir ro/_/tshangs pa nam nga lha'i bu 'dis ji skad du bstan pa'i brtson 'grus rtsom pa de lta bu dang ldan par gyur pa de'i 'og tu nga de bzhin gshegs pa mar me mdzad kyis bram ze'i khye'u khyod ma 'ongs pa'i dus na shAkya thub pa zhes bya ba de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par 
[\[89a.4\]]rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas su 'gyur ro zhes lung bstan to/_/tshangs pa de lta bas na byang chub sems dpa' lung bstan pa'i sa myur du thob par 'dod pas brtson 'grus rtsom pa 'di lta bu 'di lta ste/_chos thams cad la rtsom pa med pa'i brtson 'grus 'di la brtsam par bya'o/_/gsol 
[\[89a.5\]]pa/_bcom ldan 'das rtsom pa ma mchis pa'i brtson 'grus de gang lags/_bka' stsal pa/_tshangs pa gang dus gsum mnyam pa nyid dang ldan pa'i brtson 'grus rtsom pa'o/_/gsol pa/_bcom ldan 'das dus gsum mnyam pa nyid dang ldan pa'i brtson 'grus rtsom pa gang 
[\[89a.6\]]lags/_bka' stsal pa/_'di lta ste/_'das pa'i sems ni zad/_ma 'ongs pa ni ma phyin/_da ltar byung ba la ni gnas pa med cing mi dmigs te/_gang zad pa de ni dmigs su med do/_/gang ma byin pa de ni mi skye ba'i mtshan nyid do/_/de bzhin du gang da ltar byung ba de yang 
[\[89a.7\]]chos nyid kyis mi gnas so/_/chos nyid gang yin pa de yang ma skyes pa'o/_/gang ma skyes pa de ni 'das pa'ang ma yin/_ma 'ongs pa yang ma yin/_da ltar byung ba yang ma yin no/_/gang 'das pa'ang ma yin/_ma 'ongs pa yang ma yin/_da ltar byung ba yang ma yin pa de ni rang bzhin 
[\[89b\]]
[\[89b.1\]]no/_/rang bzhin gang yin pa de ni ma skyes pa ste/_tshangs pa dus gsum mnyam pa nyid dang ldan pa'i brtson 'grus rtsom pa de lta bu 'di ni lung bstan pa myur du thob par byed do/_/tshangs pa byang chub sems dpa' bzod pa de lta bu dang ldan pa ni chos thams cad gtong ba med pa'i 
[\[89b.2\]]sbyin pa la mos so/_/chos thams cad srung ba med pa'i tshul khrims la mos so/_/chos thams cad rlom sems med pa'i bzod pa la mos so/_/chos thams cad rtsom pa med pa'i brtson 'grus la mos so/_/chos thams cad mnyam pa nyid kyi ting nge 'dzin la mos so/_/
[\[89b.3\]]chos thams cad rnam par mi rtog pa'o zhes shes rab la mos so/_/de de ltar mos pas lhag par mi byed/_bri bar mi byed/_byed pa ma yin/_rnam par byed pa ma yin no/_/sbyin pa yang sbyin par byed la de'ang rnam par smin pa la mi re bas so/_/tshul khrims kyang srung la de'ang 
[\[89b.4\]]sgro btags pa med pas so/_/bzod pa yang sgom la de'ang phyi nang stong pa nyid kyis so/_/brtson 'grus kyang rtsom la de'ang yongs su grub pa med pas so/_/bsam gtan la yang mnyam par gzhog la de yang gnas pa med pas so/_/shes rab kyang sgom la de yang mtshan ma 
[\[89b.5\]]med pas so/_/tshangs pa byang chub sems dpa' bzod pa de lta bu dang ldan pa ni bya ba thams cad kyang ston la bya bas kyang mi gos so/_/de mi gos pas chos thams cad la mnyam pa nyid thob cing rnyed pas kyang mi 'phrogs/_/rnyed pa med pas kyang ma yin/_snyan pas kyang ma 
[\[89b.6\]]yin/_mi snyan pas kyang ma yin/_smad pas kyang ma yin/_bstod pas kyang ma yin/_sdug bsngal gyis kyang ma yin/_bde bas kyang mi 'phrogs so/_/de 'jig rten gyi chos thams cad las rnam par 'das te/_gnyis su dmigs pa'i sems dang bral zhing gnyis su med pa'i 
[\[89b.7\]]chos rtogs pa'i phyir khengs par yang mi byed/_'dud par yang mi byed/_sdud par yang mi byed/_khong khro ba yang mi byed/_gnod par yang mi byed/_kun nas gnod par yang mi byed/_rlom sems su yang mi byed/_bag med par yang mi byed/_kun nas bag med par yang mi byed 
[\[90a\]]
[\[90a.1\]]do/_/sems can thams cad yongs su smin par bya ba'i phyir lta ba gnyis su ltung ba'i sems can rnams kyi don du snying rje chen po skyed nas ched du bsam nas srid pa nye bar len te/_tshangs pa gang 'di bdag med pa'i bzod pa rab tu thob pas sems can thams cad la 
[\[90a.2\]]snying rje chen po bskyed de/_skye ba yongs su 'dzin pa 'di ni brtson 'grus rtsom pa'i mchog yin no/_/brtson 'grus brtsam pa bstan pa 'di bshad pa'i tshe byang chub sems dpa' stong phrag brgyad mi skye ba'i chos la bzod pa skyes par gyur te/_de dag thams cad kyang bcom ldan 
[\[90a.3\]]'das kyis thams cad ming gcig tu de bzhin gshegs pa brtson 'grus brtan pa zhes bya bar sangs rgyas kyi zhing tha dad par der byang chub mngon par rdzogs par 'tshang rgya bar 'gyur ro/_/zhes lung bstan to/_/de nas 'khor der tshe dang ldan pa 'od srung chen po 'dug par gyur pa 
[\[90a.4\]]des bcom ldan 'das la 'di skad ces gsol to/_/bcom ldan 'das 'di lta ste dper na/_gang klu chen po de dag ni char 'bebs na rgya mtsho chen por 'bebs te/_sa phyogs gzhan du ma lags so/_/bcom ldan 'das de bzhin du skyes bu dam pa klu chen po dang mtshungs pa 'di 
[\[90a.5\]]dag kyang chos kyi char 'di 'dra ba 'di skyes bu dam pa mtsho chen po rgya mtsho lta bu 'di dag gi sems kyi rgyud la 'bebs te/_sems can gzhan dag la ni ma lags so/_/bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/_'od srung ji skad zer ba de bzhin yin mod kyi klu'i rgyal po chen po de dag la 
[\[90a.6\]]ni gang gi phyir 'dzam bu gling 'dir char pa mi 'bebs par 'gyur ba'i ser sna med do/_/de ci'i phyir zhe na/_'dzam bu gling gi phyogs kyis char pa'i rgyun shing rta'i srog shing tsam de dag mi bzod do/_/'od srung gal te klu chen po de dag gis 'dzam bu gling 'dir char phab par gyur na des 
[\[90a.7\]]'dzam bu gling 'di grong dang /_grong khyer dang bcas/_ri dang brag dang ri bor bcas/_khor yug dang 'obs su bcas pa 'di lta ste/_rdza ma'i chus rgya shug gi lo ma bteg pa bzhin du bteg nas khyer bar 'gyur te/_de bas na klu chen po de dag 'dzam bu gling 'dir char pa mi 'bebs so/_/
[\[90b\]]
[\[90b.1\]]'od srung de bzhin du skyes bu dam pa 'di dag la ni gang gi phyir sems can de dag gzhan la chos kyi char pa chen po mi 'bebs par 'gyur ba'i chos kyi ser sna med mod kyi 'on kyang 'di dag chos 'di lta bu dag gi snod du ma gyur te/_de lta bas na skyes bu dam pa de dag rgya mtsho lta bu'i blo 
[\[90b.2\]]mchog dang ldan pa de dag la chos kyi char pa chen po 'bebs so/_/'od srung 'di lta ste dper na char pa'i rgyun shing rta'i srog shing tsam bab pa de dag gis kyang rgya mtsho chen po bri ba yang med/_gang ba yang med do/_/'od srung de bzhin du skyes bu dam pa de dag bskal pa brgyad chos nyan tam 
[\[90b.3\]]'chad kyang chos gang yang bri ba'am gang bar mi 'gyur ro/_/'di lta ste dper na rgya mtsho chen po der gling chen po bzhi'i klung sna tshogs nas gang chu 'bab pa de dag thams cad 'di lta ste/_lan tshwa'i ror ro gcig par 'gyur ro/_/'od srung de bzhin du skyes bu dam pa de dag rnam grangs 
[\[90b.4\]]sna tshogs su chos bstan pa bshad pa dag thos ma thag tu 'di lta ste/_stong pa nyid kyi ror ro gcig tu mos par 'gyur ro/_/'di lta ste dper na rgya mtsho chen po ni dag pa/_gtsang ba dri ma med pa/_gsal ba/_rnyog pa med pa yin te/_chu rnyog pa can mi 'dzin to/_/'od srung de bzhin 
[\[90b.5\]]du skyes bu dam pa de dag kyang dag pa/_gtsang ba/_dri ma med pa/_gsal ba ste/_gnod sems dang /_zhe sdang dang /_khro ba dang /_'khon du 'dzin pa thams cad mi 'dzin to/_/'di lta ste dper na rgya mtsho chen po ni zab cing gting dpag dka' ba'o/_/de bzhin du skyes bu dam pa de dag 
[\[90b.6\]]kyang chos kyi tshul zab mo la rab tu zhugs shing /_nyan thos dang /_rang sangs rgyas thams cad kyi gting dpag dka'o/_/'di lta ste dper na rgya mtsho chen po ni chu tshad med cing rin po che'i tshogs dpag tu med pa'o/_/'od srung de bzhin du skyes bu dam pa de dag kyang shes rab tshad med cing 
[\[90b.7\]]chos rin po che'i tshogs dpag tu med do/_/'od srung 'di lta ste dper na/_rgya mtsho chen po rin po che sna tshogs kyi dbyings so/_/'od srung de bzhin du skyes bu dam pa de dag kyang mngon par bsgrub pa sna tshogs kyis chos rin po che tshad med pa'i dbyings so/_/'od srung 'di lta ste dper na/_
[\[91a\]]
[\[91a.1\]]rgya mtsho chen po'i nang nas rin po che kha cig rin thang yod pa dang /_kha cig rin thang med pa dang /_kha cig mching bu lta bu dang rnam pa gsum 'byung ste/_'od srung de bzhin du skyes bu dam pa de dag sems can gyi dbang po ji lta ba bzhin du chos ston pa na yang nyan thos kyi theg 
[\[91a.2\]]pa dang /_rang sangs rgyas kyi theg pa dang /_gang 'og ma theg pa chen po la sems kyi rgyud rnam par 'grol lo/_/'od srung 'di lta ste dper na/_rgya mtsho chen po ni sems can gcig gi ched du mi gnas so/_/'od srung de bzhin du skyes bu dam pa de dag kyang sems can gcig gi don du 
[\[91a.3\]]byang chub tu sems mi skyed do/_/'od srung 'di lta ste dper na/_rgya mtsho chen po ni mthar gyis gzhol/_mthar gyis 'bab/_mthar gyis bab pa'o/_/'od srung de bzhin du skyes bu dam pa de dag kyang thams cad mkhyen pa nyid du gzhol/_thams cad mkhyen pa nyid du 'bab/_thams cad mkhyen 
[\[91a.4\]]pa nyid du bab pa'o/_/'od srung 'di lta ste dper na/_rgya mtsho chen po ni shi ba'i ro dang lhan cig mi gnas so/_/'od srung de bzhin du skyes bu dam pa de dag kyang nyan thos dang rang sangs rgyas kyi sems dang lhan cig mi gnas so/_/ser sna dang /_ngang tshul ngan pa'i sems dang /_gnod sems 
[\[91a.5\]]dang /_le lo'i sems dang /_brjed ngas pa dang /_'chal pa'i shes rab kyi sems dang lhan cig mi gnas so/_/bdag dang /_sems can dang /_srog dang /_gang zag dang /_skyes bur lta ba dang lhan cig mi gnas so/_/'od srung 'di lta ste dper na/_bskal pas sreg par gyur pa'i tshe/_
[\[91a.6\]]klung dang /_chu bran dang /_chu mig dang /_mtshe'u dang /_rdzing bu dang /_mtsho thams cad sngar skam par 'gyur te/_phyis rgya mtsho chen po zad cing bskams par 'gyur ro/_/'od srung de bzhin du dam pa'i chos nub par 'gyur ba'i tshe nyi tshe bar spyod pa la zhugs pa'i sems can thams cad kyi dam pa'i 
[\[91a.7\]]chos sngar nub par 'gyur te/_phyis skyes bu dam pa blo gros rgya mtsho lta bu sangs rgyas kyi theg pa yang dag par 'dzin pa de dag gi lag nas dam pa'i chos nub par 'gyur te/_yang 'od srung skyes bu dam pa de dag ni srog kyang yongs su gtong bar byed kyi dam pa'i chos yongs su gtong bar mi byed 
[\[91b\]]
[\[91b.1\]]na/_'od srung skyes bu dam pa de lta bu dag gi lag nas dam pa'i chos nub par 'gyur ba yod dam/_de dag gi dam pa'i chos nub par khyod kyis mi blta'o/_/'od srung 'di lta ste dper na/_rgya mtsho chen po nor bu rin po che rin po che thams cad 'dus pa zhes bya ba rdo rje tshogs kyi nor bu rin po che 
[\[91b.2\]]chen po zhig yod de/_de nyi ma bdun shar nas tshangs pa'i 'jig rten gyi bar du 'od zer byung yang rin po che de tshig par mi 'gyur te/_gang 'jig rten gyi khams kyi bar gyi sangs rgyas kyi zhing gzhan dag na rgya mtsho chen po yod pa der nor bu rin po che de der 'pho bar 'gyur te/_'od srung gang nor 
[\[91b.3\]]bu rin po che de rgya mtsho chen po las gzhan du ma 'phos pa dang /_'jig rten gyi khams 'dir tshig par mi 'gyur ba de ni gnas ma yin zhing go skabs med de/_de ni gnas med do/_/'od srung de bzhin du skyes bu dam pa de lta bu de dag kyang dam pa'i chos yongs su zad par 'gyur te/_'jig rten 
[\[91b.4\]]gnas par dam pa ma yin pa'i chos bdun byung ba'i tshe sangs rgyas kyi zhing gzhan du 'pho bar 'gyur ro/_/bdun gang zhe na/_'di lta ste/_mu stegs can gyi gnas dang /_sdig pa'i grogs pos yongs su zin pa dang /_log pa'i sbyor ba dang /_gcig la gcig 'tshe ba'i sbyor ba dang /_lta 
[\[91b.5\]]bar gyur pa'i thib po dang /_dge ba'i rtsa ba 'joms par byed pa dang /_ma thob cing mngon par ma rtogs pa ste/_dam pa ma yin pa'i chos bdun po de dag 'jig rten gnas par byung ba de na sems can rnams snod ma yin par shes nas skyes bu dam pa de dag sangs rgyas kyi zhing gzhan du 'pho bar 
[\[91b.6\]]'gyur te/_sangs rgyas mthong ba dang /_dam pa'i chos mnyan pa dang /_sems can yongs su smin par bya ba dang /_dge ba'i rtsa ba yongs su 'bar ba dang yang mi 'bral bar 'gyur ro/_/'od srung 'di lta ste dper na/_rgya mtsho chen po la brten cing sems can tshad med pa dag gnas so/_/'od srung 
[\[91b.7\]]de bzhin du byang chub sems dpa' la brten cing sems can tshad med pa dag phun sum tshogs pa gsum la gnas so/_/gsum gang zhe na/_lha'i phun sum tshogs pa dang /_mi'i phun sum tshogs pa dang /_mya ngan las 'das pa'i phun sum tshogs pa'o/_/'od srung 'di lta ste dper na/_rgya 
[\[92a\]]
[\[92a.1\]]mtsho chen po ni sems can dag gis btung bar mi nus so/_/'od srung de bzhin du skyes bu dam pa de dag gis gang chos bstan pa de dag kyang pha rol gyi rgol ba rnams kyis btung bar mi nus so/_/'od srung 'di lta ste dper na/_rgya mtsho chen po'i nang na sems can gnas pa 
[\[92a.2\]]de dag ni chu gzhan mi tshol gyi de nyid la 'thung ngo /_/'od srung de bzhin du skyes bu dam pa de dag kyang gzhan gyi ngor lta bas chos kyi ro mi 'thung gi/_rang byung gi ye shes kyi chos kyi ro 'thung ngo /_/de nas gnas brtan 'od srung chen pos bcom ldan 'das la 'di skad ces gsol to/_/bcom ldan 
[\[92a.3\]]'das stong gsum gyi stong chen po'i 'jig rten gyi khams kyi rgya mtsho chen po'i gting ngam tshad gzung ba ni nus kyi nyan thos dang /_rang sangs rgyas dang /_lha dang bcas pa'i 'jig rten gyis skyes bu dam pa de dag gi gting ngam tshad gzung ba ni mi nus te/_de bas na skyes bu dam pa de dag sems nam 
[\[92a.4\]]mkha' dang mtshungs zhes bgyi'o/_/bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/_'od srung stong gsum gyi stong chen po'i 'jig rten gyi khams gang gA'i klung gi bye ma snyed kyi rgya mtsho chen po rnams kyi gting ngam tshad ni gzung bar nus kyi nyan thos dang /_rang sangs rgyas dang /_lha dang bcas pa'i 
[\[92a.5\]]'jig rten gyi skyes bu dam pa blo gros mchog rgya mtsho lta bu dang ldan pa de dag gi gting ngam tshad gzung ba ni mi nus so/_/de nas de'i tshe bcom ldan 'das kyis tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag gsungs so/_/ji ltar chog mi shes par chu chen gter/_/'bab chu thams cad kun gyi chu tshol ba/_/de 
[\[92a.6\]]bzhin de rnams 'di la chos tshol kyang /_/rtag par chos kyis chog par 'gyur ba med/_/ji ltar ji snyed chu mang rab bsdus kyang /_/'di la rgya mtsho 'gribs shing gang 'gyur med/_/dam pa'i chos thos nas kyang chos kyi dbyings/_/shin tu 'phel shing 'grib pa mthong ba med/_/ji ltar dri med rgya 
[\[92a.7\]]mtsho chu sdud kyang /_/rnyog pa can gyi 'bab chu der mi 'bab/_/de bzhin thub mkhas spyad pa spyod pa yang /_/de dag rnam dag nyon mongs mi sdud do/_/ji ltar rgya mtsho chen po lus 'chang bas/_/zab cing gting yangs pas na mi dpogs pa/_/de bzhin yon tan rgya mtsho ye shes kyang /_/pha rol rgol ba 
[\[92b\]]
[\[92b.1\]]rnams kyis dpag mi nus/_/ji ltar rgya mtshor 'bab pa'i 'bab chu rnams/_/rnam pa sna tshogs dag kyang ro gcig 'gyur/_/de bzhin rnam pa sna tshogs chos thos nas/_/de dag rnam par grol bar ro gcig 'gyur/_/ji ltar chu 'dzin rgya mtsho chen po ni/_/skye bo gcig gi don phyir mi

[\[92b.3\]]par mi rtog rgya mtsho na/_/rtag par rin chen rnam pa gsum 'byung ste/_/de bzhin de dag chos rnams ston pa yang /_/theg pa gsum gyis sems can dag par 'gyur/_/ji ltar 'bab pa dang ni 'gram nyid nas/_/rgya mtsho chen po mthar gyis gzhol ba ltar/_/de bzhin sems can mchog rnams 
[\[92b.4\]]kun mkhyen gzhol/_/sems can rab tu dgrol phyir rtag tu brtson/_/ji ltar rgya mtsho ro dang mi gnas te/_/de dag gi ni ma 'dres chos 'di yin/_/de bzhin byang chub mchog la zhugs pa yang /_/dman pa'i theg pa dang ni gnas mi byed/_/ji ltar rgya mtsho las byung rin chen ni/_/rin chen sdud ces 
[\[92b.5\]]bya ba sra bsngags pa/_/sreg pa'i bskal pa byung yang mi tshig ste/_/de yang 'jig rten 'jig tshe gzhan du 'gro/_/de bzhin dri ma med pa'i chos 'jig tshe/_/brtson 'grus mchog ldan de dag 'bad cing 'dzin/_/snod min 'jig rten chos min 'dod rig nas/_/mkhas pa rgyal ba gzhan gyi 
[\[92b.6\]]drung du 'gro/_/nam zhig sreg pa'i bskal pa byung gyur te/_/chu mig mtsho dang lteng ka skam par 'gyur/_/rgya mtsho 'dir ni phyis nas skam par 'gyur/_/stong gsum chen po 'di yang rab tu 'jig_/nyi tshe'i spyod la nye bar gnas pa yi/_/'di la mchog tu bya ba'i chos nus pa/_/de tshe 'dir ni 
[\[92b.7\]]sems can mchog chos 'di/_/lus dang srog kyang bor nas srung bar byed/_/bde gshegs 'das na'ang rtag par bag med pas/_/de dag rnams la chos 'di mi 'jig go/_/de ltar de rnams bsam pa'ang rnam par dag_/chos la gnas shing spyod pa rtag par spyod/_/ji ltar rgya mtsho 
[\[93a\]]
[\[93a.1\]]sems can tshad med gnas/_/sems can gcig gi don du mi 'jug pa/_/de bzhin 'jig rten thams cad rnam grol phyir/_/grags pa chen po 'di dag rab tu 'jug_/'jig rten khams 'di rgya mtsho ji snyed pa/_/ma lus par ni tshad rnams shes rung yang /_/rgyal ba de

[\[93a.3\]]'grus brtul brtan zhing /_/gang dag 'jig rten ma lus dgrol ba'i phyir/_/rgya che yangs pa'i sems rab gzhol byas pa/_/'od srung de dang mtshungs med ga la lhag_/de dag rin chen 'byung gnas rgya mtsho 'dra/_/de dag bsod nams yin la bsod nams zhing /_/de dag dpyad pa'i rgyal

[\[93a.5\]]byin don mang sems pa ste/_/tshangs pa bsam gtan bzhi la gnas pa yin/_/de dag tshangs pa'i chos kyi 'khor lo bskor/_/de dag ded dpon mkhas pa 'dren pa ste/_/log pa'i lam du gnas la lam ston pa/_/de dang de dag bdud 'joms dpa' bo ste/_/sems can dam pa byang chub snying 
[\[93a.6\]]por gnas/_/de dag chos dkar tshad med zla ba bzhin/_/de dag 'od zer mngon 'phags nyi ma 'dra/_/sprin lta bur yang tshad med blo sgrogs shing /_/'jig rten gsum na bdud rtsi'i char pa 'bebs/_/de dag seng ge lta bur 'jigs skyon med/_/glang po che ltar shin tu dul ba'i sems/_/de dag ri 
[\[93a.7\]]rab ri bo 'dra mtshungs te/_/de dag pha rol rgol bas mi thub bo/_/de dag rnam par dang ba chu bzhin te/_/rtag tu bsnyen par dka' ba me dang 'dra/_/de dag rlung ltar rtag tu chags med cing /_/sa dang 'dra bar tha ba rab tu spangs/_/gnod sems nga rgyal tha ba phrag dog bral/_/de dag rtsi yi shing ltar 
[\[93b\]]
[\[93b.1\]]rtog pa med/_/'jig rten chos rnams brgyad pos ma gos shing /_/de dag tshul khrims rnam dag pad ma bzhin/_/de dag u dum bA ra'i me tog ltar/_/dge zhing bskal pa bye bar sgra thos dka'/_/de dag rkang gnyis gtso la byas pa gzo/_/de dag sangs rgyas rigs la gnas par brtson/_/

[\[93b.3\]]pa'i stobs kyis gcig tu dpa'/_/brtson 'grus rags shing rtag tu mthu dang ldan/_/de dag bsam gtan mngon par shes la mkhas/_/de dag tshad med zhing du 'phrul gyis 'gro/_/thub pa dam mchog la yang chos nyan cing /_/ji ltar thos pa dag kyang ston par byed/_/ji ltar 'di mos rnam 
[\[93b.4\]]par 'grol ba yang /_/skye bo dag gi spyod pa de dag shes/_/ji ltar mos pa mkhas shing rtogs pa ste/_/nan tan snying por byed cing mu stegs thob/_/'di rnams tshogs pa las ni 'di skye zhes/_/de dang de dag chos kun rab tu shes/_/de dag rkyen la mkhas shing rtog pa ste/_/mtha' gnyis lta

[\[93b.6\]]mi bden nyon mongs skye bo sdug bsngal te/_/de dag thar bar bya phyir spyad pa spyod/_/nga dang nga yi zhes ni rnam brtag nas/_/byis pa mi mnyam pa la brtson pas spyod/_/chos de yang dag ma yin shes nas ni/_/lta ba spangs pa'i phyir ni chos la spyod/_/bdag med bdag tu mi sdug sdug par te/_/sdug 
[\[93b.7\]]bsngal bde bar mi rtag rtag par yang /_/sems log so so'i skye bo rnams la ni/_/'khor ba'i sngon gyi mtha' yang yod ma yin/_/de dag log pa'i sbyor ba de shes nas/_/'di la sems can med dang gang zag med/_/gang la bdag sdug rtag bde med pa yin/_/yang dag sbyor ba la yang rnam par 
[\[94a\]]
